[battery_manager.cpp - C:\ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-1.8\ESP-IDF-v5.3.2\LVGL_Native_PlatformIO\src\battery_manager.cpp]

// ============================================
// BATTERY MANAGER - Implementation
// ============================================

#include "battery_manager.h"
#include "hardware_pins.h"

// Battery state
bool batteryInitialized = false;
XPowersPMU power;

bool initBattery() {
  // Initialize PMU
  if (!power.begin(Wire, AXP2101_SLAVE_ADDRESS, IIC_SDA, IIC_SCL)) {
    batteryInitialized = false;
    return false;
  }
  
  // Enable ADC measurements
  power.enableTemperatureMeasure();
  power.enableBattDetection();
  power.enableVbusVoltageMeasure();
  power.enableBattVoltageMeasure();
  power.enableSystemVoltageMeasure();
  
  Serial.println("✅ AXP2101 initialized");
  Serial.printf("   Battery: %s\n", power.isBatteryConnect() ? "Connected" : "Not detected");
  Serial.printf("   VBUS: %s\n", power.isVbusIn() ? "Connected" : "Not connected");
  
  batteryInitialized = true;
  return true;
}

String getBatteryStatus() {
  if (!batteryInitialized) {
    return "Battery: N/A";
  }
  
  String status = "";
  
  // Battery voltage and percentage
  if (power.isBatteryConnect()) {
    float voltage = power.getBattVoltage() / 1000.0;  // Convert mV to V
    int percent = power.getBatteryPercent();
    status += String(percent) + "% (" + String(voltage, 2) + "V)";
  } else {
    status += "No Battery";
  }
  
  // Charging status
  if (power.isCharging()) {
    status += " [Charging]";
  } else if (power.isVbusIn()) {
    status += " [Plugged]";
  }
  
  return status;
}

float getBatteryVoltage() {
  if (!batteryInitialized || !power.isBatteryConnect()) {
    return 0.0;
  }
  return power.getBattVoltage() / 1000.0;  // Convert mV to V
}

int getBatteryPercent() {
  if (!batteryInitialized || !power.isBatteryConnect()) {
    return -1;
  }
  return power.getBatteryPercent();
}

bool isCharging() {
  if (!batteryInitialized) {
    return false;
  }
  return power.isCharging();
}

bool isVbusConnected() {
  if (!batteryInitialized) {
    return false;
  }
  return power.isVbusIn();
}

[button_manager.cpp - C:\ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-1.8\ESP-IDF-v5.3.2\LVGL_Native_PlatformIO\src\button_manager.cpp] 
============================================ 
#include "button_manager.h"
#include <Arduino.h>

// Pin definitions
#define BUTTON_TOP_PIN 0
#define BUTTON_BOTTOM_EXPANDER_PIN 4

// Timing constants (milliseconds)
#define DEBOUNCE_MS 50
#define HOLD_RECORD_MS 500       // 0.5s for record hold
#define HOLD_POWER_MS 5000       // 5s for power off
#define CLICK_TIMEOUT_MS 1000    // Max time between clicks for triple-click (allow slower clicks)

// Button state structure
typedef struct {
    bool current_state;              // Current raw reading
    bool last_stable_state;          // Last debounced state
    unsigned long state_change_time; // When state last changed
    unsigned long press_start_time;  // When button was pressed
    unsigned long release_time;      // When button was released
    int click_count;                 // Number of clicks in sequence
    unsigned long first_click_time;  // Time of first click in sequence
    bool hold_record_fired;          // Has 0.5s hold event fired?
    bool hold_power_fired;           // Has 5s hold event fired?
    bool is_holding;                 // Currently in hold state (not clicking)
} button_state_t;

// Global state
static button_state_t top_button = {HIGH, HIGH, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, false, false, false};
static button_state_t bottom_button = {HIGH, HIGH, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, false, false, false};
static button_callback_t event_callback = nullptr;
static ESP_IOExpander *expander = nullptr;

bool button_manager_init(button_callback_t callback, ESP_IOExpander *io_exp) {
    event_callback = callback;
    expander = io_exp;
    
    // Initialize top button (GPIO0)
    pinMode(BUTTON_TOP_PIN, INPUT_PULLUP);
    Serial.println("✅ Button Manager: Top button (GPIO0) initialized");
    
    // Bottom button is configured by the IO expander during display init
    if (expander) {
        Serial.println("✅ Button Manager: Bottom button (Expander Pin 4) ready");
    } else {
        Serial.println("⚠️  Button Manager: No IO Expander provided, bottom button disabled");
    }
    
    return true;
}

static void update_button(button_id_t id, button_state_t *btn, bool raw_state) {
    unsigned long now = millis();
    
    // Update current state
    if (raw_state != btn->current_state) {
        btn->current_state = raw_state;
        btn->state_change_time = now;
        return;  // Wait for debounce period
    }
    
    // Check if debounce period has elapsed
    if (now - btn->state_change_time < DEBOUNCE_MS) {
        return;  // Still in debounce period
    }
    
    // Stable state detected - check if different from last known state
    if (btn->current_state != btn->last_stable_state) {
        btn->last_stable_state = btn->current_state;
        
        if (btn->current_state == LOW) {
            // ===== BUTTON PRESSED =====
            btn->press_start_time = now;
            btn->hold_record_fired = false;
            btn->hold_power_fired = false;
            btn->is_holding = false;
        } else {
            // ===== BUTTON RELEASED =====
            btn->release_time = now;
            unsigned long press_duration = now - btn->press_start_time;
            
            if (btn->is_holding) {
                // Released after hold - fire RELEASE event and don't count as click
                if (event_callback) {
                    event_callback(id, BUTTON_EVENT_RELEASE);
                }
                btn->is_holding = false;
                btn->click_count = 0;  // Reset click counter
            } else {
                // Quick press/release - count as click
                btn->click_count++;
                
                if (btn->click_count == 1) {
                    btn->first_click_time = now;
                } else if (btn->click_count == 3) {
                    // Triple click detected!
                    if (event_callback && id == BUTTON_TOP) {
                        event_callback(id, BUTTON_EVENT_TRIPLE_CLICK);
                    }
                    btn->click_count = 0;  // Reset
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Check for hold events (only while pressed)
    if (btn->last_stable_state == LOW) {
        unsigned long hold_time = now - btn->press_start_time;
        
        // Check 5s hold first (for power off on bottom button)
        if (!btn->hold_power_fired && hold_time >= HOLD_POWER_MS) {
            btn->hold_power_fired = true;
            btn->is_holding = true;
            if (event_callback && id == BUTTON_BOTTOM) {
                event_callback(id, BUTTON_EVENT_HOLD_POWER);
            }
        }
        // Check 0.5s hold (for recording on top button)
        else if (!btn->hold_record_fired && hold_time >= HOLD_RECORD_MS) {
            btn->hold_record_fired = true;
            btn->is_holding = true;
            if (event_callback && id == BUTTON_TOP) {
                event_callback(id, BUTTON_EVENT_HOLD_RECORD);
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Check for single click timeout
    if (btn->click_count > 0 && btn->click_count < 3) {
        if (now - btn->first_click_time > CLICK_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            // Timeout expired, fire single click
            if (event_callback) {
                event_callback(id, BUTTON_EVENT_CLICK);
            }
            btn->click_count = 0;  // Reset
        }
    }
}

bool button_manager_update() {
    // Update top button (direct GPIO)
    bool top_raw = digitalRead(BUTTON_TOP_PIN);
    update_button(BUTTON_TOP, &top_button, top_raw);
    
    // Update bottom button (via IO expander)
    // NOTE: This button has REVERSED logic! HIGH=pressed, LOW=released
    // We invert it here so the rest of the code can treat it normally (LOW=pressed)
    if (expander) {
        bool bottom_raw = expander->digitalRead(BUTTON_BOTTOM_EXPANDER_PIN);
        bool bottom_inverted = !bottom_raw;  // Invert: HIGH->LOW (pressed), LOW->HIGH (released)
        update_button(BUTTON_BOTTOM, &bottom_button, bottom_inverted);
    }
    
    return true;
}

bool button_is_pressed(button_id_t button) {
    if (button == BUTTON_TOP) {
        return top_button.last_stable_state == LOW;
    } else {
        return bottom_button.last_stable_state == LOW;
    }
}

// Runtime certificate (loaded from NVS or defaults to DEFAULT_ROOT_CA)
static char* g_root_ca = nullptr;

/**
 * Load Root CA certificate from NVS or use hardcoded default
 * Call this once during initialization
 */
static void loadRootCA() {
    // Allocate buffer for certificate (4KB max)
    const size_t CERT_BUFFER_SIZE = 4096;
    static char cert_buffer[CERT_BUFFER_SIZE];
    
    // Try to load from NVS first
    if (NVS::loadRootCA(cert_buffer, CERT_BUFFER_SIZE)) {
        Serial.println("🔐 Using Root CA from NVS (custom)");
        g_root_ca = cert_buffer;
    } else {
        Serial.println("🔐 Using default hardcoded Root CA");
        g_root_ca = (char*)DEFAULT_ROOT_CA;
    }
}

/**
 * Get current Root CA certificate
 */
static const char* getRootCA() {
    if (!g_root_ca) {
        loadRootCA();
    }
    return g_root_ca;
}

// =====================================================
// ====== CONSTANTS / LIMITS ===========================
// =====================================================

static const size_t   MAX_REPLY_WAV = 2048 * 1024; // cap ~2MB for TTS reply (~64s @ 16kHz mono)

// =====================================================
// ====== AUDIO BUFFER INTEGRATION ======================
// =====================================================
//
// Audio manager provides:
//  - audio_get_recording_wav(): Returns pointer to complete WAV in PSRAM
//    (includes 44B header + PCM data), or nullptr if not available
//  - audio_free_recording_buffer(): Frees the buffer after upload

// =====================================================
// ====== RAM DEBUG HELPER =============================
// =====================================================

static void logRam(const char *stage) {
  multi_heap_info_t internal;
  multi_heap_info_t external;
  heap_caps_get_info(&internal, MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL);
  heap_caps_get_info(&external, MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM);

  Serial.printf(
    "[RAM][%s] internal_free=%u largest_block=%u | psram_free=%u largest_block=%u\n",
    stage,
    (unsigned)internal.total_free_bytes,
    (unsigned)internal.largest_free_block,
    (unsigned)external.total_free_bytes,
    (unsigned)external.largest_free_block
  );
}

// =====================================================
// ====== UTILS (HEX, TIME, URL PARSING) ===============
// =====================================================

static String toHex(const uint8_t *data, size_t len) {
  static const char *hexchars = "0123456789abcdef";
  String out;
  out.reserve(len * 2);
  for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    uint8_t b = data[i];
    out += hexchars[(b >> 4) & 0x0F];
    out += hexchars[b & 0x0F];
  }
  return out;
}

// UTC "YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ"
static String iso8601Now() {
  time_t now = time(nullptr);
  struct tm tm_utc;
  gmtime_r(&now, &tm_utc);

  char buf[32];
  snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf),
           "%04d-%02d-%02dT%02d:%02d:%02dZ",
           tm_utc.tm_year + 1900,
           tm_utc.tm_mon + 1,
           tm_utc.tm_mday,
           tm_utc.tm_hour,
           tm_utc.tm_min,
           tm_utc.tm_sec);
  return String(buf);
}

// HMAC-SHA256 (secret is hex string, server does bytes.fromhex)
static String hmac_sha256_hex_hexKey(const char *hexKey, const String &message) {
  size_t hexLen = strlen(hexKey);
  size_t keyLen = hexLen / 2;
  uint8_t *keyBytes = (uint8_t *)malloc(keyLen);
  if (!keyBytes) {
    Serial.println("!! malloc keyBytes failed");
    return "";
  }

  auto hexToNibble = [](char c)->uint8_t {
    if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') return (uint8_t)(c - '0');
    if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') return (uint8_t)(10 + (c - 'a'));
    if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F') return (uint8_t)(10 + (c - 'A'));
    return 0;
  };

  for (size_t i = 0; i < keyLen; i++) {
    char hi = hexKey[i * 2];
    char lo = hexKey[i * 2 + 1];
    keyBytes[i] = (hexToNibble(hi) << 4) | hexToNibble(lo);
  }

  uint8_t hmacOut[32];
  mbedtls_md_context_t ctx;
  mbedtls_md_init(&ctx);
  const mbedtls_md_info_t *info = mbedtls_md_info_from_type(MBEDTLS_MD_SHA256);

  if (mbedtls_md_setup(&ctx, info, 1) != 0) {
    Serial.println("!! mbedtls_md_setup failed");
    mbedtls_md_free(&ctx);
    free(keyBytes);
    return "";
  }

  mbedtls_md_hmac_starts(&ctx, keyBytes, keyLen);
  mbedtls_md_hmac_update(&ctx,
                         (const unsigned char *)message.c_str(),
                         message.length());
  mbedtls_md_hmac_finish(&ctx, hmacOut);

  mbedtls_md_free(&ctx);
  free(keyBytes);

  return toHex(hmacOut, sizeof(hmacOut));
}

// Extract "audio_url":"..." or detect "audio_url":null
static bool extractAudioUrl(const String &json, String &outUrl) {
  int keyPos = json.indexOf("\"audio_url\"");
  if (keyPos < 0) return false;

  int colonPos = json.indexOf(":", keyPos);
  if (colonPos < 0) return false;

  // Check if value is null (not a string)
  int checkStart = colonPos + 1;
  while (checkStart < json.length() && (json[checkStart] == ' ' || json[checkStart] == '\t')) {
    checkStart++;  // Skip whitespace
  }
  
  // If next non-whitespace char is 'n', check for "null"
  if (checkStart < json.length() && json[checkStart] == 'n') {
    if (json.substring(checkStart, checkStart + 4) == "null") {
      return false;  // audio_url is null
    }
  }

  // Value should be a string - find quotes
  int firstQ = json.indexOf("\"", colonPos);
  if (firstQ < 0) return false;

  int secondQ = json.indexOf("\"", firstQ + 1);
  if (secondQ < 0) return false;

  outUrl = json.substring(firstQ + 1, secondQ);
  if (outUrl.length() == 0) return false;
  
  return true;
}

// Split "https://host/path..." into hostOut="host", pathOut="/path..."
static bool splitUrlHostPath(const String &url, String &hostOut, String &pathOut) {
  String work = url;
  const String prefix = "https://";
  if (work.startsWith(prefix)) {
    work.remove(0, prefix.length());
  }
  int slashPos = work.indexOf('/');
  if (slashPos < 0) return false;
  hostOut = work.substring(0, slashPos);
  pathOut = work.substring(slashPos);
  return true;
}

// Split global serverUrl (e.g. "https://your-server.example.com/transcribe")
static bool splitServerUrl(String &hostOut, String &pathOut) {
  return splitUrlHostPath(serverUrl, hostOut, pathOut);
}

// =====================================================
// ====== TLS DOWNLOAD (TTS WAV) =======================
// =====================================================
//
// downloadAudio(url, savePath):
// - Downloads TTS audio from URL to PSRAM buffer (g_replyBuf)
// - Saves to SD card at savePath for audio playback
// - Returns true if download succeeded (even if SD save failed)
//

bool downloadAudio(const char* url, const char* savePath) {
  String sUrl(url ? url : "");
  Serial.println("🔽 Starting downloadAudio...");
  Serial.printf("🔽 URL: %s\n", sUrl.c_str());
  // logRam("beforeDownload");

  // Parse target host/path
  String host, path;
  if (!splitUrlHostPath(sUrl, host, path)) {
    Serial.println("❌ splitUrlHostPath failed");
    return false;
  }

  // Reset SD card flags
  g_ttsOnSdCard = false;
  g_ttsSdCardPath = "";

  // Allocate PSRAM buffer once
  if (!g_replyBuf) {
    g_replyBuf = (uint8_t *)heap_caps_malloc(MAX_REPLY_WAV, MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM);
    if (!g_replyBuf) {
      Serial.println("❌ PSRAM alloc failed for reply buffer");
      return false;
    }
  }
  g_replyLen = 0;

  WiFiClientSecure client;
  client.setCACert(getRootCA());
  client.setTimeout(5000);

  Serial.printf("🔐 TLS GET connect to %s ...\n", host.c_str());
  if (!client.connect(host.c_str(), 443)) {
    Serial.println("❌ TLS connect() failed (download)");
    return false;
  }

  // Send GET
  client.printf(
    "GET %s HTTP/1.1\r\n"
    "Host: %s\r\n"
    "Connection: close\r\n"
    "\r\n",
    path.c_str(),
    host.c_str()
  );

  // Read headers
  String header;
  bool headerDone = false;
  unsigned long lastRead = millis();
  size_t contentLen = 0;  // Content-Length from server (needed for progress tracking)
  
  // Track last LVGL refresh for 24fps updates
  unsigned long last_lvgl_refresh = millis();

  while (!headerDone && (millis() - lastRead < 5000)) {
    while (client.available()) {
      char c = client.read();
      if (c < 0) break;
      header += c;
      lastRead = millis();

      // Refresh LVGL at 24fps during header read
      unsigned long now = millis();
      if (now - last_lvgl_refresh >= 42) {
        lv_refr_now(NULL);
        last_lvgl_refresh = now;
      }
      
      int idx = header.indexOf("\r\n\r\n");
      if (idx >= 0) {
        String bodyStart = header.substring(idx + 4);
        header = header.substring(0, idx);

        // status code
        int statusCode = -1;
        {
          int sp1 = header.indexOf(' ');
          int sp2 = header.indexOf(' ', sp1 + 1);
          if (sp1 >= 0 && sp2 > sp1) {
            String codeStr = header.substring(sp1 + 1, sp2);
            statusCode = codeStr.toInt();
          }
        }
        Serial.printf("🔽 Download HTTP status: %d\n", statusCode);

        // Content-Length (optional)
        {
          int clPos = header.indexOf("Content-Length:");
          if (clPos >= 0) {
            int lineEnd = header.indexOf("\r\n", clPos);
            String line;
            if (lineEnd > clPos) {
              line = header.substring(clPos, lineEnd);
            } else {
              line = header.substring(clPos);
            }
            int colon = line.indexOf(':');
            if (colon >= 0) {
              String num = line.substring(colon + 1);
              num.trim();
              contentLen = (size_t) num.toInt();
            }
          }
        }
        Serial.printf("🔽 Declared Content-Length: %u\n", (unsigned)contentLen);
        
        // Check if file is too large for PSRAM - save to SD card instead
        if (contentLen > MAX_REPLY_WAV) {
          Serial.printf("⚠️  File too large for PSRAM (%u KB > %u KB)\n", 
                       (unsigned)(contentLen / 1024), (unsigned)(MAX_REPLY_WAV / 1024));
          Serial.println("💾 Automatically saving to SD card...");
          
          // Generate unique filename for SD card
          g_ttsSdCardPath = "/sdcard/tts/response_" + String(millis()) + ".wav";
          
          // Ensure /tts directory exists
          if (!SD.exists("/sdcard/tts")) {
            Serial.println("📁 Creating /sdcard/tts directory...");
            if (!SD.mkdir("/sdcard/tts")) {
              Serial.println("❌ Failed to create /sdcard/tts directory");
              client.stop();
              return false;
            }
          }
          
          // Open SD file for writing
          File sdFile = SD.open(g_ttsSdCardPath.c_str(), FILE_WRITE);
          if (!sdFile) {
            Serial.printf("❌ Failed to open %s for writing\n", g_ttsSdCardPath.c_str());
            client.stop();
            return false;
          }
          
          // Write initial body data to SD
          if (bodyStart.length() > 0) {
            sdFile.write((const uint8_t*)bodyStart.c_str(), bodyStart.length());
            g_replyLen = bodyStart.length();
          }
          
          Serial.printf("💾 Streaming to SD: %s\n", g_ttsSdCardPath.c_str());
          
          // Stream rest of body directly to SD card
          uint8_t chunk[1024];
          size_t last_progress_update = g_replyLen;
          
          headerDone = true;
          
          while (client.connected() || client.available()) {
            // Check for cancel
            if (g_upload_cancel) {
              Serial.println("⚠️ Download cancelled by flag");
              sdFile.close();
              client.stop();
              SD.remove(g_ttsSdCardPath.c_str());  // Delete incomplete file
              return false;
            }
            
            size_t available = client.available();
            if (available > 0) {
              size_t toRead = available > sizeof(chunk) ? sizeof(chunk) : available;
              size_t bytesRead = client.read(chunk, toRead);
              if (bytesRead > 0) {
                sdFile.write(chunk, bytesRead);
                g_replyLen += bytesRead;
                
                // Update progress every 16KB
                if ((g_replyLen - last_progress_update) >= (16 * 1024)) {
                  int kb_data[2] = {(int)(g_replyLen / 1024), (int)(contentLen / 1024)};
                  Serial.printf("📊 Download: Progress %d kb / %d kb (%.1f%%)\n", 
                               kb_data[0], kb_data[1], 
                               100.0 * ((double)g_replyLen / (double)contentLen));
                  display_manager_update_data(kb_data);
                  last_progress_update = g_replyLen;
                }
                
                // Refresh LVGL at 24fps
                unsigned long now = millis();
                if (now - last_lvgl_refresh >= 42) {
                  lv_refr_now(NULL);
                  last_lvgl_refresh = now;
                }
              }
            }
          }
          
          sdFile.close();
          client.stop();
          
          // Final progress
          int kb_data[2] = {(int)(g_replyLen / 1024), (int)(contentLen / 1024)};
          Serial.printf("📊 Download: Complete %d kb / %d kb (100%%)\n", kb_data[0], kb_data[1]);
          display_manager_update_data(kb_data);
          
          Serial.printf("✅ Saved large TTS to SD: %s (%u bytes)\n", g_ttsSdCardPath.c_str(), (unsigned)g_replyLen);
          g_ttsOnSdCard = true;
          return true;
        }

        // Normal PSRAM path - copy bodyStart into PSRAM
        int bodyLen0 = bodyStart.length();
        if ((size_t)bodyLen0 > MAX_REPLY_WAV) {
          bodyLen0 = (int)MAX_REPLY_WAV;
        }
        memcpy(g_replyBuf, bodyStart.c_str(), bodyLen0);
        g_replyLen = bodyLen0;

        headerDone = true;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  if (!headerDone) {
    Serial.println("❌ header not received / timeout");
    client.stop();
    return false;
  }

  // Initial progress update (show header bytes received)
  if (contentLen > 0) {
    int kb_data[2] = {(int)(g_replyLen / 1024), (int)(contentLen / 1024)};
    Serial.printf("\r📊 Download: %d / %d kb (0%%)  ", kb_data[0], kb_data[1]);
    Serial.flush();
    display_manager_update_data(kb_data);
  }

  // Slurp rest of body with progress updates
  size_t last_progress_update = g_replyLen;
  while (client.connected() || client.available()) {
    while (client.available()) {
      int c = client.read();
      if (c < 0) break;
      if (g_replyLen < MAX_REPLY_WAV) {
        g_replyBuf[g_replyLen++] = (uint8_t)c;
      }
    }
    
    // Update progress every 16KB
    if (contentLen > 0 && (g_replyLen - last_progress_update) >= (16 * 1024)) {
      int kb_data[2] = {(int)(g_replyLen / 1024), (int)(contentLen / 1024)};
      Serial.printf("\r📊 Download: %d / %d kb (%.0f%%)  ", 
                    kb_data[0], kb_data[1], 
                    100.0 * ((double)g_replyLen / (double)contentLen));
      Serial.flush();
      display_manager_update_data(kb_data);
      last_progress_update = g_replyLen;
    }
    
    // Refresh LVGL at 24fps during download
    unsigned long now = millis();
    if (now - last_lvgl_refresh >= 42) {
      lv_refr_now(NULL);
      last_lvgl_refresh = now;
    }
  }
  
  // Final progress update
  if (contentLen > 0) {
    int kb_data[2] = {(int)(g_replyLen / 1024), (int)(contentLen / 1024)};
    Serial.printf("\r📊 Download: %d / %d kb (100%%) ✓\n", kb_data[0], kb_data[1]);
    display_manager_update_data(kb_data);
  }

  client.stop();

  Serial.printf("🔽 Download complete, got %u bytes into g_replyBuf\n",
                (unsigned)g_replyLen);

  // dump first ~32 bytes hex
  size_t dumpN = (g_replyLen < 32) ? g_replyLen : 32;
  Serial.print("🔽 First bytes: ");
  for (size_t i = 0; i < dumpN; i++) {
    Serial.printf("%02X ", g_replyBuf[i]);
  }
  Serial.println();

  // Save to SD card if savePath provided
  if (savePath && g_replyLen > 0) {
    // Verify SD card is mounted
    if (SD.cardType() == CARD_NONE) {
      Serial.println("❌ SD card not mounted - cannot save TTS file");
      return true;  // Download succeeded, just couldn't save
    }
    
    // Ensure /tts directory exists
    if (!SD.exists("/tts")) {
      Serial.println("📁 Creating /tts directory...");
      if (!SD.mkdir("/tts")) {
        Serial.println("❌ Failed to create /tts directory");
        return true;  // Download succeeded, just couldn't save
      }
    }
    
    File f = SD.open(savePath, "w");
    if (f) {
      size_t written = f.write(g_replyBuf, g_replyLen);
      f.close();
      if (written == g_replyLen) {
        Serial.printf("💾 Saved TTS to: %s (%u bytes)\n", savePath, (unsigned)written);
      } else {
        Serial.printf("⚠️  Partial write to %s (%u/%u bytes)\n", savePath, (unsigned)written, (unsigned)g_replyLen);
      }
    } else {
      Serial.printf("❌ Failed to open %s for writing\n", savePath);
    }
  }

  // logRam("afterDownload");
  return true;
}

// =====================================================
// ====== CORE MULTIPART UPLOAD ========================
// =====================================================
//
// doMultipartUpload():
// - opens TLS
// - POST multipart/form-data with entire WAV
// - reads JSON
// - extracts audio_url
// - calls downloadAudio() to pull TTS
//

static bool doMultipartUpload(
    const uint8_t *wavData,
    size_t wavSize,
    ServerResponse &outResp
) {
  outResp = {};

  if (!wavData || wavSize == 0) {
    Serial.println("❌ No wavData");
    outResp.success = false;
    outResp.httpCode = -1;
    outResp.errorMessage = "No wavData";
    return false;
  }

  // Parse serverUrl into host+path
  String host, path;
  if (!splitServerUrl(host, path)) {
    Serial.println("❌ Bad serverUrl format");
    outResp.success = false;
    outResp.httpCode = -1;
    outResp.errorMessage = "Bad serverUrl";
    return false;
  }

  // Device auth
  // Get device secret from settings_manager (stored in NVS)
  String deviceSecret = settings_manager_get_device_secret();

  if (deviceId.length() == 0) {
    deviceId = getMacAddress();
  }

  String deviceTime     = iso8601Now();
  String signingMessage = deviceId + "|" + deviceTime;
  String signatureHex   = hmac_sha256_hex_hexKey(deviceSecret.c_str(), signingMessage);

Serial.printf("Device-ID: %s\n", deviceId.c_str());
Serial.printf("Device-Time: %s\n", deviceTime.c_str());
Serial.printf("Signature (HMAC256): %s\n", signatureHex.c_str());

// Get current conversation ID (if any) from state machine
extern const char* state_machine_get_conversation_id();
const char* convId = state_machine_get_conversation_id();
bool hasContinuation = (convId && strlen(convId) > 0);

// Build multipart boundaries
String preBody;
preBody.reserve(512);  // Increased for conversation_id field

// Add conversation_id field if we're continuing a conversation
if (hasContinuation) {
Serial.printf("🔄 Continuing conversation: %s\n", convId);
preBody += "--";
preBody += BOUNDARY;
preBody += "\r\n";
preBody += "Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"conversation_id\"\r\n";
preBody += "\r\n";
preBody += convId;
preBody += "\r\n";
}

  // Audio file part
  preBody += "--";
  preBody += BOUNDARY;
  preBody += "\r\n";
  preBody += "Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"audio\"; filename=\"rec.wav\"\r\n";
  preBody += "Content-Type: audio/wav\r\n";
  preBody += "\r\n";

  String postBody = "\r\n--";
  postBody += BOUNDARY;
  postBody += "--\r\n";

  size_t contentLength = preBody.length() + wavSize + postBody.length();

  // TLS
  WiFiClientSecure client;
  client.setCACert(getRootCA());
  client.setTimeout(5000);

  // logRam("preConnect");
  Serial.printf("🔐 TLS connect to %s:443 ...\n", host.c_str());
  if (!client.connect(host.c_str(), 443)) {
    Serial.println("❌ TLS connect() failed");
    outResp.success = false;
    outResp.httpCode = -1;
    outResp.errorMessage = "TLS connect failed";
    return false;
  }
  // logRam("postConnect");

  // HTTP headers
  client.printf(
    "POST %s HTTP/1.1\r\n"
    "Host: %s\r\n"
    "Content-Type: multipart/form-data; boundary=%s\r\n"
    "Content-Length: %u\r\n"
    "Connection: close\r\n"
    "X-Device-ID: %s\r\n"
    "X-Device-Time: %s\r\n"
    "X-Device-Signature: %s\r\n"
    "\r\n",
    path.c_str(),
    host.c_str(),
    BOUNDARY,
    (unsigned)contentLength,
    deviceId.c_str(),
    deviceTime.c_str(),
    signatureHex.c_str()
  );

  // multipart prefix
  client.print(preBody);

  // stream WAV in CHUNK_SIZE slices
  size_t sent = 0;
  size_t offset = 0;
  
  // Track last LVGL refresh for 24fps updates (used in upload and response loops)
  unsigned long last_lvgl_refresh = millis();
  
  // Initial progress update (0 kb)
  int kb_data[2] = {0, (int)(wavSize / 1024)};
  Serial.printf("\r📊 Upload: 0 / %d kb (0%%)  ", kb_data[1]);
  Serial.flush();
  display_manager_update_data(kb_data);
  
  while (offset < wavSize) {
    if (g_upload_cancel) {
      Serial.println("⚠️ Upload cancelled by flag");
      break;
    }

    size_t n = wavSize - offset;
    if (n > CHUNK_SIZE) n = CHUNK_SIZE;

    size_t written = client.write(wavData + offset, n);
    offset += written;
    sent   += written;
    
    // Refresh LVGL at 24fps (every 42ms) for smooth spinner animation
    unsigned long now = millis();
    if (now - last_lvgl_refresh >= 42) {
      lv_refr_now(NULL);
      last_lvgl_refresh = now;
    }

    // progress log & update every 16KB (more frequent updates)
    if ((sent % (16 * 1024)) < CHUNK_SIZE) {
      // Update display with progress in KB
      int kb_data[2] = {
        (int)(sent / 1024),
        (int)(wavSize / 1024)
      };
      // Single line progress with carriage return
      Serial.printf("\r📊 Upload: %d / %d kb (%.0f%%)  ",
                    kb_data[0], kb_data[1],
                    100.0 * ((double)sent / (double)wavSize));
      Serial.flush();
      display_manager_update_data(kb_data);
    }
  }
  
  // Final progress update (complete)
  kb_data[0] = (int)(sent / 1024);
  kb_data[1] = (int)(wavSize / 1024);
  display_manager_update_data(kb_data);
  Serial.printf("\r📊 Upload: %d / %d kb (100%%) ✓\n",
                kb_data[0], kb_data[1]);

  // multipart tail
  client.print(postBody);
  sent += postBody.length();

  // read full response with timeout and cancel support
  client.setTimeout(10000);  // 10s socket timeout
  String rawResp;
  unsigned long responseStart = millis();
  const unsigned long RESPONSE_TIMEOUT = 90000;  // 90 second timeout (matches server)
  
  while (client.connected() || client.available()) {
    // Check for timeout
    if (millis() - responseStart > RESPONSE_TIMEOUT) {
      Serial.println("⚠️  Response timeout - closing connection");
      break;
    }
    
    // Check for cancel
    if (g_upload_cancel) {
      Serial.println("⚠️  Upload cancelled during response read");
      break;
    }
    
    while (client.available()) {
      char c = client.read();
      if (c < 0) break;
      rawResp += c;
    }
    
    // Refresh LVGL at 24fps during response wait
    unsigned long now = millis();
    if (now - last_lvgl_refresh >= 42) {
      lv_refr_now(NULL);
      last_lvgl_refresh = now;
    }
    
    delay(1);  // Yield to other tasks
  }
  client.stop();
  
  // Check if cancelled or timed out
  if (g_upload_cancel) {
    outResp.success = false;
    outResp.httpCode = -1;
    outResp.errorMessage = "Upload cancelled";
    return false;
  }
  
  if (rawResp.length() == 0) {
    Serial.println("❌ No response received from server");
    outResp.success = false;
    outResp.httpCode = -1;
    outResp.errorMessage = "No response (timeout or connection closed)";
    return false;
  }

  // separate headers/body
  int hdrEnd = rawResp.indexOf("\r\n\r\n");
  String headerPart;
  String bodyPart;
  if (hdrEnd >= 0) {
    headerPart = rawResp.substring(0, hdrEnd);
    bodyPart   = rawResp.substring(hdrEnd + 4);
  } else {
    headerPart = rawResp;
    bodyPart   = "";
  }

  // status code
  int statusCode = -1;
  {
    int sp1 = headerPart.indexOf(' ');
    int sp2 = headerPart.indexOf(' ', sp1 + 1);
    if (sp1 >= 0 && sp2 > sp1) {
      String codeStr = headerPart.substring(sp1 + 1, sp2);
      statusCode = codeStr.toInt();
    }
  }

  Serial.printf("🌍 Server HTTP status: %d\n", statusCode);
  Serial.println("🌍 Response body:");
  Serial.println(bodyPart);
  // logRam("afterUpload");

  // fill outResp
  outResp.httpCode  = statusCode;
  outResp.success   = (statusCode == 200);
  outResp.errorMessage = outResp.success ? "" : "HTTP error";

  // Extract response type (say/ask/confirm)
  outResp.type = RESPONSE_UNKNOWN;
  {
    int k = bodyPart.indexOf("\"type\"");
    if (k >= 0) {
      int cpos = bodyPart.indexOf(":", k);
      int q1   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", cpos);
      int q2   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", q1 + 1);
      if (cpos >= 0 && q1 >= 0 && q2 > q1) {
        String typeStr = bodyPart.substring(q1 + 1, q2);
        if (typeStr == "say") {
          outResp.type = RESPONSE_SAY;
        } else if (typeStr == "ask") {
          outResp.type = RESPONSE_ASK;
        } else if (typeStr == "confirm") {
          outResp.type = RESPONSE_CONFIRM;
        }
        Serial.printf("🔍 Response type: %s\n", typeStr.c_str());
      }
    }
  }

  // Extract conversation_id (for multi-turn)
  {
    int k = bodyPart.indexOf("\"conversation_id\"");
    if (k >= 0) {
      int cpos = bodyPart.indexOf(":", k);
      int q1   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", cpos);
      int q2   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", q1 + 1);
      if (cpos >= 0 && q1 >= 0 && q2 > q1) {
        outResp.conversationId = bodyPart.substring(q1 + 1, q2);
        Serial.printf("🔍 Conversation ID: %s\n", outResp.conversationId.c_str());
      }
    }
  }

  // Extract transcription
  {
    int k = bodyPart.indexOf("\"transcription\"");
    if (k >= 0) {
      int cpos = bodyPart.indexOf(":", k);
      int q1   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", cpos);
      int q2   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", q1 + 1);
      if (cpos >= 0 && q1 >= 0 && q2 > q1) {
        outResp.transcription = bodyPart.substring(q1 + 1, q2);
      }
    }
  }

  // Extract message (the response text)
  {
    int k = bodyPart.indexOf("\"message\"");
    if (k >= 0) {
      int cpos = bodyPart.indexOf(":", k);
      int q1   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", cpos);
      int q2   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", q1 + 1);
      if (cpos >= 0 && q1 >= 0 && q2 > q1) {
        outResp.message = bodyPart.substring(q1 + 1, q2);
        Serial.printf("🔍 Message: %s\n", outResp.message.c_str());
      }
    }
  }

  // Extract command (for confirm type)
  {
    int k = bodyPart.indexOf("\"command\"");
    if (k >= 0) {
      int cpos = bodyPart.indexOf(":", k);
      int q1   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", cpos);
      int q2   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", q1 + 1);
      if (cpos >= 0 && q1 >= 0 && q2 > q1) {
        outResp.command = bodyPart.substring(q1 + 1, q2);
        Serial.printf("🔍 Command: %s\n", outResp.command.c_str());
      }
    }
  }

  // Extract TTS URL (download will happen later in main.cpp)
  {
    String ttsUrl;
    if (extractAudioUrl(bodyPart, ttsUrl)) {
      outResp.ttsUrl = ttsUrl;
      Serial.printf("🔎 Found audio_url: %s\n", ttsUrl.c_str());
      Serial.println("   ℹ️  TTS URL stored - download will happen in DOWNLOADING state");
    } else {
      Serial.println("ℹ️  No audio_url in response (null or missing) - no TTS to download");
    }
  }

  return outResp.success;
}

// MAIN ENTRY: upload most recent recording.
// 1. Try direct PSRAM buffer from recorder (no SD).
// 2. If not available, fall back to opening the file from SD.
ServerResponse transcribeAudio(const char* audioFilePath) {
  ServerResponse respOut = {};

  // Fast path: use in-PSRAM WAV from recorder
  size_t psramLen = 0;
  uint8_t *psramBuf = audio_get_recording_wav(&psramLen);
  
  if (psramBuf != nullptr && psramLen > 0) {
    Serial.printf(
      "📤 transcribeAudio(): Using direct PSRAM buffer (%u bytes), skipping SD reload\n",
      (unsigned)psramLen
    );

    bool ok = doMultipartUpload(psramBuf, psramLen, respOut);
    if (!ok) {
      Serial.println("❌ doMultipartUpload() failed (PSRAM path)");
    }
    // We DO NOT call audio_free_recording_buffer() here.
    // Your queue logic decides when to free.
    return respOut;
  }

  // Slow path: pull file from SD into PSRAM (for retries / queue)
  if (!audioFilePath) {
    respOut.success = false;
    respOut.httpCode = -1;
    respOut.errorMessage = "null path";
    Serial.println("❌ transcribeAudio(): null path and no PSRAM buffer");
    return respOut;
  }

  Serial.printf(
    "📤 transcribeAudio(): No PSRAM buffer, loading from SD: %s\n",
    audioFilePath
  );

  // Use POSIX file API (keep full /sdcard path)
  FILE* f = openFileForRead(audioFilePath);
  if (!f) {
    Serial.printf("❌ Failed to open %s\n", audioFilePath);
    respOut.success = false;
    respOut.httpCode = -1;
    respOut.errorMessage = "open fail";
    return respOut;
  }

  // Get file size
  fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
  size_t sz = ftell(f);
  fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET);
  
  uint8_t *buf = (uint8_t*) heap_caps_malloc(sz, MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM);
  if (!buf) {
    Serial.println("❌ PSRAM alloc failed for WAV file");
    fclose(f);
    respOut.success = false;
    respOut.httpCode = -1;
    respOut.errorMessage = "psram alloc fail";
    return respOut;
  }

  size_t rd = fread(buf, 1, sz, f);
  fclose(f);
  if (rd != sz) {
    Serial.println("❌ short read");
    heap_caps_free(buf);
    respOut.success = false;
    respOut.httpCode = -1;
    respOut.errorMessage = "short read";
    return respOut;
  }

  Serial.printf(
    "📤 transcribeAudio(): loaded %u bytes from SD into PSRAM\n",
    (unsigned)sz
  );

  bool ok = doMultipartUpload(buf, sz, respOut);
  heap_caps_free(buf);

  if (!ok) {
    Serial.println("❌ doMultipartUpload() failed (SD path)");
  }
  return respOut;
}

// Direct "here's a buffer in RAM already" path. Useful for queue retries
// that already staged the WAV in memory.
ServerResponse transcribeAudioFromBuffer(const uint8_t* wavData,
                                         size_t size,
                                         const char* logFilename)
{
  (void)logFilename;
  ServerResponse respOut = {};

  Serial.printf(
    "📤 transcribeAudioFromBuffer(): size=%u (%s)\n",
    (unsigned)size,
    logFilename ? logFilename : "no-name"
  );

  doMultipartUpload(wavData, size, respOut);
  return respOut;
}

// Confirmation / cancel / heartbeat / etc. are currently stubs so
// the rest of your firmware links and runs.
// You can wire them to actual endpoints later.

// Execute confirmed command (POST /execute)
ServerResponse executeCommand(const char* conversationId, const char* command) {
  ServerResponse r = {};
  
  if (!conversationId || !command) {
    r.success = false;
    r.httpCode = -1;
    r.errorMessage = "Missing conversationId or command";
    Serial.println("❌ executeCommand: null params");
    return r;
  }

  // Parse serverUrl to extract just the host (ignore path - /execute is absolute)
  String host, path;
  if (!splitServerUrl(host, path)) {
    Serial.println("❌ Bad serverUrl format");
    r.success = false;
    r.httpCode = -1;
    r.errorMessage = "Bad serverUrl";
    return r;
  }

  // Device auth
  String deviceSecret = settings_manager_get_device_secret();
  if (deviceId.length() == 0) {
    deviceId = getMacAddress();
  }

  String deviceTime = iso8601Now();
  String signingMessage = deviceId + "|" + deviceTime;
  String signatureHex = hmac_sha256_hex_hexKey(deviceSecret.c_str(), signingMessage);

  // Build JSON body
  String jsonBody = "{\"command\":\"" + String(command) + "\",\"conversation_id\":\"" + String(conversationId) + "\"}";
  
  // TLS
  WiFiClientSecure client;
  client.setCACert(getRootCA());
  client.setTimeout(10000);

  Serial.printf("🔐 TLS connect to %s:443 for /execute...\n", host.c_str());
  if (!client.connect(host.c_str(), 443)) {
    Serial.println("❌ TLS connect() failed");
    r.success = false;
    r.httpCode = -1;
    r.errorMessage = "TLS connect failed";
    return r;
  }

  // POST /execute (absolute path, not relative to transcribe)
  String executePath = "/execute";

  client.printf(
    "POST %s HTTP/1.1\r\n"
    "Host: %s\r\n"
    "Content-Type: application/json\r\n"
    "Content-Length: %u\r\n"
    "Connection: close\r\n"
    "X-Device-ID: %s\r\n"
    "X-Device-Time: %s\r\n"
    "X-Device-Signature: %s\r\n"
    "\r\n",
    executePath.c_str(),
    host.c_str(),
    jsonBody.length(),
    deviceId.c_str(),
    deviceTime.c_str(),
    signatureHex.c_str()
  );

  client.print(jsonBody);

  // Read response
  String rawResp;
  unsigned long start = millis();
  while (client.connected() || client.available()) {
    if (millis() - start > 10000) break;  // 10s timeout
    while (client.available()) {
      char c = client.read();
      if (c < 0) break;
      rawResp += c;
    }
    delay(1);
  }
  client.stop();

  Serial.println("🌍 /execute response:");
  Serial.println(rawResp);

  // Parse status code
  int statusCode = -1;
  int hdrEnd = rawResp.indexOf("\r\n\r\n");
  String headerPart = hdrEnd >= 0 ? rawResp.substring(0, hdrEnd) : rawResp;
  String bodyPart = hdrEnd >= 0 ? rawResp.substring(hdrEnd + 4) : "";

  int sp1 = headerPart.indexOf(' ');
  int sp2 = headerPart.indexOf(' ', sp1 + 1);
  if (sp1 >= 0 && sp2 > sp1) {
    String codeStr = headerPart.substring(sp1 + 1, sp2);
    statusCode = codeStr.toInt();
  }

  r.httpCode = statusCode;
  r.success = (statusCode == 200);
  r.conversationId = conversationId;
  r.errorMessage = r.success ? "" : "Execute failed";
  r.type = RESPONSE_SAY;  // Execute always returns SAY response

  if (r.success && bodyPart.length() > 0) {
    Serial.println("🔍 Parsing /execute response JSON...");
    
    // Extract message
    {
      int k = bodyPart.indexOf("\"message\"");
      if (k >= 0) {
        int cpos = bodyPart.indexOf(":", k);
        int q1   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", cpos);
        int q2   = bodyPart.indexOf("\"", q1 + 1);
        if (cpos >= 0 && q1 >= 0 && q2 > q1) {
          r.message = bodyPart.substring(q1 + 1, q2);
          Serial.printf("🔍 Message: %s\n", r.message.c_str());
        }
      }
    }
    
    // Extract TTS URL
    {
      String ttsUrl;
      if (extractAudioUrl(bodyPart, ttsUrl)) {
        r.ttsUrl = ttsUrl;
        Serial.printf("🔎 Found audio_url: %s\n", ttsUrl.c_str());
      }
    }
  }

  Serial.printf("✅ Execute command '%s' - HTTP %d\n", command, statusCode);
  return r;
}

const uint8_t* getTtsBuffer(size_t* outSize) {
  // If TTS was saved to SD card (too large for PSRAM), return nullptr
  // Caller should check isTtsOnSdCard() and use getTtsSdCardPath() instead
  if (g_ttsOnSdCard) {
    if (outSize) {
      *outSize = 0;
    }
    return nullptr;
  }
  
  if (outSize) {
    *outSize = g_replyLen;
  }
  return g_replyBuf;
}

bool isTtsOnSdCard() {
  return g_ttsOnSdCard;
}

const char* getTtsSdCardPath() {
  return g_ttsSdCardPath.c_str();
}

size_t getTtsSize() {
  return g_replyLen;
}

void freeTtsBuffers() {
  // Free PSRAM buffer if allocated
  if (g_replyBuf) {
    free(g_replyBuf);
    g_replyBuf = nullptr;
    Serial.printf("🧹 Freed TTS PSRAM buffer (%d KB)\n", g_replyLen / 1024);
  }
  
  // Delete SD file if TTS was saved there
  if (g_ttsOnSdCard && g_ttsSdCardPath.length() > 0) {
    if (SD.remove(g_ttsSdCardPath.c_str())) {
      Serial.printf("🧹 Deleted TTS SD file: %s\n", g_ttsSdCardPath.c_str());
    }
  }
  
  // Reset state
  g_replyLen = 0;
  g_ttsOnSdCard = false;
  g_ttsSdCardPath = "";
}

  return WiFi.macAddress();

  if (deviceMac.length() == 0) {
    deviceMac = WiFi.macAddress();
  }
  return deviceMac;
}

// Some other modules still use HTTPClient and expect us to attach
// auth headers. We keep this helper compatible with that.
void addAuthHeaders(HTTPClient &http, bool includeContentType) {
  (void)includeContentType;

  if (deviceId.length() == 0) {
    deviceId = getMacAddress();
  }

  // Get device secret from settings_manager (stored in NVS)
  String deviceSecret = settings_manager_get_device_secret();

  String nowIso = iso8601Now();
  String signingMessage = deviceId + "|" + nowIso;
  String sigHex = hmac_sha256_hex_hexKey(deviceSecret.c_str(), signingMessage);

  http.addHeader("X-Device-ID",        deviceId);
  http.addHeader("X-Device-Time",      nowIso);
  http.addHeader("X-Device-Signature", sigHex);

  // Caller can still add Content-Type etc.
}

#include "imu_manager.h"

// QMI8658_L_SLAVE_ADDRESS is defined in SensorQMI8658.hpp

// Global sensor instance
static SensorQMI8658 qmi;
static IMUdata acc = {0, 0, 0};
static IMUdata gyr = {0, 0, 0};
static bool initialized = false;

namespace IMU {

bool init(TwoWire &wire, int sda, int scl) {
    Serial.println("🎯 Initializing IMU (QMI8658)...");
    
    if (qmi.begin(wire, QMI8658_L_SLAVE_ADDRESS, sda, scl)) {
        Serial.println("✅ QMI8658 initialized");
        
        // Configure accelerometer: ±4g range, 1000Hz sampling
        qmi.configAccelerometer(
            SensorQMI8658::ACC_RANGE_4G,
            SensorQMI8658::ACC_ODR_1000Hz
        );
        
        // Configure gyroscope: ±64dps range, ~900Hz sampling
        qmi.configGyroscope(
            SensorQMI8658::GYR_RANGE_64DPS,
            SensorQMI8658::GYR_ODR_896_8Hz
        );
        
        // Enable both sensors
        qmi.enableAccelerometer();
        qmi.enableGyroscope();
        
        initialized = true;
        Serial.println("   Accelerometer: ±4g, 1000Hz");
        Serial.println("   Gyroscope: ±64dps, 896Hz");
        
        return true;
    } else {
        Serial.println("❌ QMI8658 initialization failed!");
        initialized = false;
        return false;
    }
}

bool isReady() {
    return initialized;
}

bool update() {
    if (!initialized) return false;
    
    if (qmi.getDataReady()) {
        qmi.getAccelerometer(acc.x, acc.y, acc.z);
        qmi.getGyroscope(gyr.x, gyr.y, gyr.z);
        return true;
    }
    
    return false;
}

bool getAccelerometer(IMUdata &data) {
    if (!initialized) return false;
    
    data = acc;
    return true;
}

bool getGyroscope(IMUdata &data) {
    if (!initialized) return false;
    
    data = gyr;
    return true;
}

float getAccelerometerMagnitude() {
    if (!initialized) return 0.0f;
    
    return sqrt(acc.x * acc.x + acc.y * acc.y + acc.z * acc.z);
}

bool detectShake(float threshold) {
    if (!initialized) return false;
    
    float magnitude = getAccelerometerMagnitude();
    
    // Shake detected if total acceleration > threshold
    // (normal gravity is 1g, so >2g indicates movement)
    return (magnitude > threshold);
}

int getOrientation() {
    if (!initialized) return 0;
    
    // Determine orientation based on which axis has strongest gravity
    float abs_x = abs(acc.x);
    float abs_y = abs(acc.y);
    float abs_z = abs(acc.z);
    
    // Device is flat if Z-axis dominates
    if (abs_z > abs_x && abs_z > abs_y) {
        return 0;  // Flat (face up or down)
    }
    
    // Portrait/landscape based on X/Y dominance
    if (abs_y > abs_x) {
        return acc.y > 0 ? 1 : 4;  // 1=portrait, 4=upside_down
    } else {
        return acc.x > 0 ? 2 : 3;  // 2=landscape_left, 3=landscape_right
    }
}

void printValues() {
    if (!initialized) {
        Serial.println("IMU not initialized");
        return;
    }
    
    Serial.printf("Accelerometer (g):  X=%.2f  Y=%.2f  Z=%.2f  |  Mag=%.2f\n",
                  acc.x, acc.y, acc.z, getAccelerometerMagnitude());
    Serial.printf("Gyroscope (dps):    X=%.1f  Y=%.1f  Z=%.1f\n",
                  gyr.x, gyr.y, gyr.z);
}

} // namespace IMU

// LVGL DISPLAY FLUSH CALLBACK
// ============================================
void lvgl_flush_callback(lv_disp_drv_t *disp, const lv_area_t *area, lv_color_t *color_p) {
    uint32_t w = (area->x2 - area->x1 + 1);
    uint32_t h = (area->y2 - area->y1 + 1);
    
    if (gfx) {
        gfx->draw16bitRGBBitmap(area->x1, area->y1, (uint16_t *)&color_p->full, w, h);
    }
    
    lv_disp_flush_ready(disp);
}

// ============================================
// LVGL TOUCH READ CALLBACK
// ============================================
void lvgl_touch_callback(lv_indev_drv_t *indev_driver, lv_indev_data_t *data) {
    if (touch && touch->IIC_Interrupt_Flag) {
        touch->IIC_Interrupt_Flag = false;
        
        int32_t touchX = touch->IIC_Read_Device_Value(touch->Arduino_IIC_Touch::Value_Information::TOUCH_COORDINATE_X);
        int32_t touchY = touch->IIC_Read_Device_Value(touch->Arduino_IIC_Touch::Value_Information::TOUCH_COORDINATE_Y);
        
        data->state = LV_INDEV_STATE_PR;
        data->point.x = touchX;
        data->point.y = touchY;
    } else {
        data->state = LV_INDEV_STATE_REL;
    }
}

}

// ============================================
// STATE MACHINE CALLBACK
// ============================================
void state_change_callback(app_state_t old_state, app_state_t new_state) {
    // State change already logged by state_machine.cpp, just update display
    
    // Note: Galaxy is automatically destroyed when IDLE screen is deleted
    // (display_manager now deletes IDLE screen on state transition)
    
    // Update display based on new state
    switch (new_state) {
        case STATE_IDLE: {
            // Check if queue has files - if yes, show queue screen instead of IDLE
            if (queue_size() > 0) {
                Serial.printf("📋 Queue has %d files - showing queue screen\n", queue_size());
                display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED);
                
                // Update queue screen with filenames list
                String filenames = queue_get_filenames_list();
                display_manager_update_queue_filenames(filenames.c_str());
                
                // Update WiFi state for button visibility
                display_manager_update_queue_wifi(isWiFiConnected());
            } else {
                display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_IDLE);
            }
            break;
        }
        case STATE_RECORDING:
            display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_RECORDING);
            break;
        case STATE_UPLOADING:
            display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_UPLOADING);
            break;
        case STATE_DOWNLOADING:
            display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_DOWNLOADING);
            break;
        case STATE_PLAYING:
            Serial.println("   📺 Display: Switching to PLAYING screen (SAY mode - green)");
            display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING);
            break;
        case STATE_PLAYING_ASK:
            Serial.println("   📺 Display: Switching to PLAYING_ASK screen (yellow)");
            display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_ASK);
            break;
        case STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM:
            Serial.println("   📺 Display: Switching to PLAYING_CONFIRM screen (purple)");
            display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM);
            break;
        case STATE_QUEUED: {
            display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED);
            
            // Update queue screen with filenames list
            String filenames = queue_get_filenames_list();
            display_manager_update_queue_filenames(filenames.c_str());
            
            // Update WiFi state for button visibility
            display_manager_update_queue_wifi(isWiFiConnected());
            break;
        }
        case STATE_SETTINGS:
            // Note: Settings handled by button callback
            break;
        case STATE_SLEEP:
            // Note: Sleep handled by sleep_manager
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
}

// ============================================
// FORWARD DECLARATIONS
// ============================================
void display_event_callback(display_state_t from_state, int event_data);

// ============================================
// BUTTON EVENT CALLBACK
// ============================================
void button_event_callback(button_id_t button, button_event_t event) {
    const char* btn_name = (button == BUTTON_TOP) ? "TOP" : "BOTTOM";
    const char* evt_name = "UNKNOWN";
    
    switch (event) {
        case BUTTON_EVENT_CLICK:        evt_name = "CLICK"; break;
        case BUTTON_EVENT_TRIPLE_CLICK: evt_name = "TRIPLE_CLICK"; break;
        case BUTTON_EVENT_HOLD_RECORD:  evt_name = "HOLD_RECORD"; break;
        case BUTTON_EVENT_HOLD_POWER:   evt_name = "HOLD_POWER"; break;
        case BUTTON_EVENT_RELEASE:      evt_name = "RELEASE"; break;
    }
    
    Serial.printf("🔘 Button: %s %s\n", btn_name, evt_name);
    
    // Update activity timestamp
    state_machine_update_activity();
    
    // Check if sleep manager wants to handle this (wake from sleep)
    if (sleep_manager_handle_button(button, event)) {
        return;  // Sleep manager handled it (device woke up)
    }
    
    // Handle button events based on current state
    app_state_t current_state = state_machine_get();
    
    if (button == BUTTON_TOP) {
        if (event == BUTTON_EVENT_CLICK) {
            // CONFIRM state: Execute the command
            if (current_state == STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM) {
                Serial.println("   → Top button: Confirming command execution");
                
                // Get conversation data from state machine
                const char* convId = state_machine_get_conversation_id();
                
                // Execute the command (need to store command from pending_response)
                // We'll use a static variable to store the command from server response
                extern String g_pending_command;  // Declared below
                
                if (g_pending_command.length() > 0 && convId && strlen(convId) > 0) {
                    Serial.printf("   📤 Executing command: %s\n", g_pending_command.c_str());
                    
                    // Call executeCommand
                    ServerResponse execResp = executeCommand(convId, g_pending_command.c_str());
                    
                    // Check if server returned audio (success OR failure)
                    if (execResp.ttsUrl.length() > 0) {
                        // Server returned a response - download and play it (even for errors)
                        if (execResp.success) {
                            Serial.println("   ✅ Command executed - downloading response");
                        } else {
                            Serial.println("   ❌ Command failed - downloading error explanation");
                        }
                        
                        pending_tts_url = execResp.ttsUrl;
                        pending_response_text = execResp.message;
                        g_playing_text_ver++;  // Mark text as dirty/updated
                        pending_response_type = RESPONSE_SAY;  // Execute always returns SAY
                        pending_conversation_reset = true;  // Flag to reset after playback
                        
                        Serial.printf("   📝 Success response message: '%s'\n", pending_response_text.c_str());
                        Serial.println("   ℹ️  This message will be displayed on the PLAYING screen");
                        Serial.println("   🔄 Conversation will reset after response plays");
                        
                        state_machine_set(STATE_DOWNLOADING);
                    } else if (execResp.success) {
                        // Success but no audio response - return to idle
                        Serial.println("   ✅ Command executed successfully (no audio)");
                        
                        // Reset conversation (command completed)
                        state_machine_reset_conversation();
                        Serial.println("   🔄 Conversation reset (command completed)");
                        
                        if (queue_size() > 0) {
                            state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                        } else {
                            state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
                        }
                    } else {
                        // Execution failed with no audio - return to idle
                        Serial.println("   ❌ Command execution failed (no audio)");
                        
                        // Reset conversation (start fresh)
                        state_machine_reset_conversation();
                        Serial.println("   🔄 Conversation reset (execution failed)");
                        
                        if (queue_size() > 0) {
                            state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                        } else {
                            state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
                        }
                    }
                    
                    g_pending_command = "";  // Clear after use
                } else {
                    Serial.println("   ⚠️  No command or conversation ID to execute");
                    state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
                }
                return;
            }
            
            // Queue screen: Trigger upload
            if (current_state == STATE_QUEUED) {
                Serial.println("   → Top button: Upload selected recording");
                // Trigger upload event (same as touch button)
                display_event_callback(DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED, QUEUE_EVENT_PROCESS);
                return;
            }
            
            // Other states: No action
            Serial.println("   → Top click (no action in this state)");
        } else if (event == BUTTON_EVENT_TRIPLE_CLICK) {
            // Enter settings (only from IDLE or QUEUED states)
            if (current_state == STATE_IDLE || current_state == STATE_QUEUED) {
                Serial.println("   → Entering SETTINGS mode");
                state_machine_set(STATE_SETTINGS);
                display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_SETTINGS);
                
                // Small delay to ensure LVGL is ready
                lv_timer_handler();
                delay(10);
                
                // Load and set current values
                display_manager_set_settings_values(
                    settings_manager_get_brightness(),
                    settings_manager_get_volume(),
                    settings_manager_get_mic_gain()
                );
            } else {
                Serial.printf("   ⚠️  Cannot enter settings from state: %s\n", state_machine_get_name(current_state));
            }
        } else if (event == BUTTON_EVENT_HOLD_RECORD) {
            // Start recording (hold detected)
            // Allow from IDLE, QUEUED, or PLAYING states (for corrections/multi-turn)
            if (current_state == STATE_IDLE || 
                current_state == STATE_QUEUED ||
                current_state == STATE_PLAYING ||
                current_state == STATE_PLAYING_ASK ||
                current_state == STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM) {
                
                // Cancel SAY screen linger timer (if active)
                if (say_screen_linger_start != 0) {
                    Serial.println("   ⏱️  SAY screen linger cancelled by user recording");
                    say_screen_linger_start = 0;
                }
                
                // Check if we have enough PSRAM for new recording
                if (!queue_can_record()) {
                    Serial.println("   ❌ Cannot record: Queue full (PSRAM < 4MB free)");
                    Serial.println("   💡 Wait for queued uploads to complete");
                    // TODO: Show visual feedback to user
                    return;
                }
                
                // Capture origin state before entering RECORDING
                g_state_before_recording = current_state;
                Serial.printf("   → Starting recording (origin: %s)\n", state_machine_get_name(g_state_before_recording));
                
                state_machine_set(STATE_RECORDING);
                
                // Start audio recording
                if (audio_start_recording()) {
                    Serial.println("   ✅ Audio recording started");
                } else {
                    Serial.println("   ❌ Failed to start audio recording");
                    // Return to appropriate state (check if queue has items)
                    if (queue_size() > 0 && g_state_before_recording == STATE_IDLE) {
                        state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                    } else {
                        state_machine_set(g_state_before_recording);
                    }
                }
            }
        } else if (event == BUTTON_EVENT_RELEASE) {
            // Stop recording (button released)
            if (current_state == STATE_RECORDING) {
                Serial.println("   → Stopping recording (released)");
                
                // Get filename before stopping
                String filename = audio_get_recording_filename();
                
                // Stop audio recording
                if (audio_stop_recording()) {
                    if (filename.length() > 0) {
                        Serial.printf("   ✅ Recording saved: %s\n", filename.c_str());
                    } else {
                        Serial.println("   ✅ Recording ready in PSRAM (SD not available)");
                    }
                    
                    // Handle based on origin state AND WiFi status
                    if ((g_state_before_recording == STATE_IDLE ||
                         g_state_before_recording == STATE_PLAYING_ASK ||
                         g_state_before_recording == STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM) &&
                        g_state_before_recording != STATE_QUEUED) {
                        // From IDLE/ASK/CONFIRM: Auto-upload (continue conversation)
                        Serial.println("   → Transitioning to UPLOADING (conversation continues)");
                        state_machine_set(STATE_UPLOADING);
                        state_machine_set_recording(filename.c_str());
                        // Upload happens in loop (non-blocking)
                    } else if (g_state_before_recording == STATE_QUEUED) {
                        // From QUEUE: Add to queue and return to QUEUE
                        Serial.println("   → Origin was QUEUE: adding to queue and returning to QUEUE");
                        
                        // Get recording data
                        size_t wavSize = 0;
                        uint8_t* wavData = audio_get_recording_wav(&wavSize);
                        
                        if (wavData && wavSize > 0) {
                            if (queue_enqueue_recording(wavData, wavSize, filename.c_str())) {
                                Serial.println("   ✅ Added to queue (new recording at end)");
                                audio_free_recording_buffer();
                                Serial.println("   🧹 Freed recording PSRAM buffer");
                                
                                // Refresh queue display
                                String filenames = queue_get_filenames_list();
                                display_manager_update_queue_filenames(filenames.c_str());
                            } else {
                                Serial.println("   ❌ Failed to add to queue");
                            }
                        }
                        
                        // Return to QUEUE state
                        state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                    } else {
                        // Fallback: transition to UPLOADING (shouldn't happen)
                        Serial.println("   ⚠️  Unknown origin state: defaulting to UPLOADING");
                        state_machine_set(STATE_UPLOADING);
                        state_machine_set_recording(filename.c_str());
                    }
                } else {
                    Serial.println("   ❌ Failed to stop audio recording");
                    // Return to appropriate state (check if queue has items)
                    if (queue_size() > 0 && g_state_before_recording == STATE_IDLE) {
                        state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                    } else {
                        state_machine_set(g_state_before_recording);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    if (button == BUTTON_BOTTOM) {
        if (event == BUTTON_EVENT_CLICK) {
            // SAY state: Cancel linger and return to IDLE immediately
            if (current_state == STATE_PLAYING) {
                Serial.println("   → Bottom button: Canceling SAY screen (returning to IDLE)");
                
                // Stop audio playback immediately
                if (audio_is_playing()) {
                    audio_stop_playback();
                    Serial.println("   ⏸️  Audio playback stopped");
                }
                
                // Free TTS buffers to recover PSRAM
                freeTtsBuffers();
                
                // Cancel linger timer
                if (say_screen_linger_start != 0) {
                    Serial.println("   ⏱️  SAY screen linger cancelled by button");
                    say_screen_linger_start = 0;
                }
                
                // Give system time to clean up audio before state transition
                delay(10);
                lv_refr_now(NULL);  // Process any pending LVGL updates
                
                // Return to appropriate state
                if (queue_size() > 0) {
                    state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                } else {
                    state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
                }
                return;
            }
            
            // CONFIRM state: Abort command execution
            if (current_state == STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM) {
                Serial.println("   → Bottom button: Aborting command (returning to IDLE)");
                extern String g_pending_command;
                g_pending_command = "";  // Clear pending command
                
                // Free TTS buffers to recover PSRAM
                freeTtsBuffers();
                
                // Reset conversation (start fresh next time)
                state_machine_reset_conversation();
                Serial.println("   🔄 Conversation reset (next recording starts fresh)");
                
                // Return to appropriate state
                if (queue_size() > 0) {
                    state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                } else {
                    state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
                }
                return;
            }
            
            // ASK state: Abort/skip (return to IDLE)
            if (current_state == STATE_PLAYING_ASK) {
                Serial.println("   → Bottom button: Aborting ASK (returning to IDLE)");
                
                // Free TTS buffers to recover PSRAM
                freeTtsBuffers();
                
                // Reset conversation (start fresh next time)
                state_machine_reset_conversation();
                Serial.println("   🔄 Conversation reset (next recording starts fresh)");
                
                // Return to appropriate state
                if (queue_size() > 0) {
                    state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                } else {
                    state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
                }
                return;
            }
            
            // Queue screen: Delete selected recording
            if (current_state == STATE_QUEUED) {
                Serial.println("   → Bottom button: Delete selected recording");
                // Trigger delete event (same as touch button)
                display_event_callback(DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED, QUEUE_EVENT_DELETE_ALL);
                return;
            }
            
            // Cancel UPLOADING/DOWNLOADING
            if (current_state == STATE_UPLOADING || current_state == STATE_DOWNLOADING) {
                Serial.println("   → Bottom button: Canceling network operation");
                extern volatile bool g_upload_cancel;  // Defined in https_client.cpp
                g_upload_cancel = true;
                return;
            }
            
            // Cancel current operation
            if (current_state == STATE_RECORDING) {
                Serial.println("   → Canceling recording");
                
                // Stop recording (file will be saved but not uploaded)
                if (audio_stop_recording()) {
                    Serial.println("   ✅ Recording canceled (file discarded)");
                    // TODO: Optionally delete the file here
                }
                
                // Return to appropriate state (check if queue has items)
                if (queue_size() > 0 && g_state_before_recording == STATE_IDLE) {
                    Serial.println("   → Queue has files - returning to QUEUED");
                    state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                } else {
                    Serial.printf("   → Returning to origin state: %s\n", state_machine_get_name(g_state_before_recording));
                    state_machine_set(g_state_before_recording);
                }
                
            } else if (current_state == STATE_UPLOADING || 
                       current_state == STATE_DOWNLOADING || 
                       current_state == STATE_PLAYING ||
                       current_state == STATE_PLAYING_ASK ||
                       current_state == STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM) {
                // Cancel upload/download/playback
                Serial.println("   → Canceling operation");
                
                // Signal upload to abort
                extern volatile bool g_upload_cancel;
                g_upload_cancel = true;
                
                // Clear pending command if in CONFIRM state
                extern String g_pending_command;
                g_pending_command = "";
                
                // Return to appropriate state
                if (queue_size() > 0) {
                    state_machine_set(STATE_QUEUED);
                } else {
                    state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
                }
                
            } else if (current_state != STATE_IDLE) {
                Serial.println("   → Canceling, return to IDLE");
                state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
            }
        } else if (event == BUTTON_EVENT_HOLD_POWER) {
            // Power off (enter sleep)
            Serial.println("   → Power off (entering SLEEP)");
            sleep_manager_enter_sleep();
        }
    }
}

// ============================================
// HELPER: Create confirmation dialog
// ============================================
static lv_obj_t* create_confirmation_dialog(const char* title, const char* message,
                                            lv_event_cb_t yes_cb, lv_event_cb_t no_cb) {
    // Create modal background
    lv_obj_t* bg = lv_obj_create(lv_scr_act());
    lv_obj_set_size(bg, LV_PCT(100), LV_PCT(100));
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(bg, lv_color_black(), 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_opa(bg, LV_OPA_70, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_border_width(bg, 0, 0);
    lv_obj_clear_flag(bg, LV_OBJ_FLAG_SCROLLABLE);
    
    // Create dialog container
    lv_obj_t* dialog = lv_obj_create(bg);
    lv_obj_set_size(dialog, 280, 180);
    lv_obj_center(dialog);
    lv_obj_set_style_radius(dialog, 12, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(dialog, lv_color_hex(0x2C2C2C), 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_border_color(dialog, lv_color_hex(0x505050), 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_border_width(dialog, 2, 0);
    lv_obj_clear_flag(dialog, LV_OBJ_FLAG_SCROLLABLE);  // Disable left/right scrolling
    
    // Title
    lv_obj_t* title_label = lv_label_create(dialog);
    lv_label_set_text(title_label, title);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(title_label, lv_color_white(), 0);
    lv_obj_align(title_label, LV_ALIGN_TOP_MID, 0, 12);
    
    // Message
    lv_obj_t* msg_label = lv_label_create(dialog);
    lv_label_set_text(msg_label, message);
    lv_obj_set_width(msg_label, 250);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_align(msg_label, LV_TEXT_ALIGN_CENTER, 0);
    lv_obj_set_style_text_color(msg_label, lv_color_hex(0xCCCCCC), 0);
    lv_obj_align(msg_label, LV_ALIGN_TOP_MID, 0, 45);
    
    // No button (left)
    lv_obj_t* btn_no = lv_btn_create(dialog);
    lv_obj_set_size(btn_no, 110, 40);
    lv_obj_align(btn_no, LV_ALIGN_BOTTOM_LEFT, 10, -10);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(btn_no, lv_color_hex(0x505050), 0);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(btn_no, no_cb, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);
    lv_obj_t* label_no = lv_label_create(btn_no);
    lv_label_set_text(label_no, "No");
    lv_obj_center(label_no);
    
    // Yes button (right)
    lv_obj_t* btn_yes = lv_btn_create(dialog);
    lv_obj_set_size(btn_yes, 110, 40);
    lv_obj_align(btn_yes, LV_ALIGN_BOTTOM_RIGHT, -10, -10);
    lv_obj_set_style_bg_color(btn_yes, lv_color_hex(0xCC3333), 0);  // Red for destructive action
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(btn_yes, yes_cb, LV_EVENT_CLICKED, NULL);
    lv_obj_t* label_yes = lv_label_create(btn_yes);
    lv_label_set_text(label_yes, "Yes");
    lv_obj_center(label_yes);
    
    return bg;
}

// ============================================
// DELETE CONFIRMATION DIALOG CALLBACKS
// ============================================
static void delete_confirm_yes_cb(lv_event_t* e) {
    Serial.println("📋 Delete confirmed (Yes clicked)");
    
    // Close dialog FIRST (before state transitions) to prevent freeze
    if (g_delete_msgbox) {
        lv_obj_del_async(g_delete_msgbox);
        g_delete_msgbox = nullptr;
    }
    
    if (g_delete_all_pending) {
        Serial.println("   ✅ Deleting all queue files");
        queue_delete_all();
        g_delete_all_pending = false;
        g_queue_delete_confirmation = false;
        
        // Return to IDLE (queue is now empty)
        state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
        display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_IDLE);  // Update display to show IDLE screen
    } else if (g_delete_pending_filename.length() > 0) {
        Serial.printf("   ✅ Deleting: %s\n", g_delete_pending_filename.c_str());
        
        // Delete using sd_manager (POSIX API)
        if (deleteFile(g_delete_pending_filename.c_str())) {
            Serial.println("   ✅ File deleted from SD");
            
            // Remove from in-memory queue
            queue_remove_from_memory(g_delete_pending_filename);
            
            // Refresh queue UI
            String filenames = queue_get_filenames_list();
            display_manager_update_queue_filenames(filenames.c_str());
            
            // If queue is now empty, return to IDLE
            if (queue_size() == 0) {
                Serial.println("   📋 Queue now empty - returning to IDLE");
                state_machine_set(STATE_IDLE);
                display_manager_set_state(DISPLAY_STATE_IDLE);  // Update display to show IDLE screen
            }
        } else {
            Serial.println("   ❌ Failed to delete file from SD");
        }
        
        g_delete_pending_filename = "";
    }
}

static void delete_confirm_no_cb(lv_event_t* e) {
    Serial.println("📋 Delete canceled (No clicked)");
    g_delete_all_pending = false;
    g_delete_pending_filename = "";
    g_queue_delete_confirmation = false;
    
    // Close dialog
    if (g_delete_msgbox) {
        lv_obj_del_async(g_delete_msgbox);
        g_delete_msgbox = nullptr;
    }
}

// ============================================
// DISPLAY EVENT CALLBACK (from display_manager)
// ============================================
void display_event_callback(display_state_t from_state, int event_data) {
    Serial.printf("🎯 Touch event: state=%d, data=%d\n", from_state, event_data);
    
    // Update activity timestamp (touch is activity!)
    state_machine_update_activity();
    
    if (from_state == DISPLAY_STATE_IDLE) {
        if (event_data == 1) {
            Serial.println("   🎤 Mic icon touched (not used - physical button only)");
        } else if (event_data == 2) {
            Serial.println("   ⏻ Power icon touched");
            sleep_manager_enter_sleep();
        }
    }
    
    // Queue screen events
    else if (from_state == DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED) {
        Serial.printf("   Queue event: %d\n", event_data);
        
        if (event_data == QUEUE_EVENT_PLAY) {
            // Play button clicked - play selected recording INLINE (stay in QUEUED)
            Serial.println("   PLAY button clicked");
            
            // Get selected recording from queue screen
            lv_obj_t* queue_screen = display_manager_get_queue_screen();
            String filename = "";
            int selected_index = 1;
            
            if (queue_screen) {
                selected_index = screen_queue_get_selected_index(queue_screen);
                filename = screen_queue_get_selected_filename(queue_screen);
                
                if (selected_index == 0) {
                    // "All recordings" selected - enable sequential playback
                    Serial.println("   → Playing ALL recordings (auto-advance enabled)");
                    g_auto_advance_playback = true;
                    g_current_playback_index = 1;  // Start with first file
                    
                    // Get items list to find first file
                    auto items = queue_get_items_info();
                    if (!items.empty()) {
                        filename = items[0].filename;
                        // Update queue screen selection to highlight first file
                        if (queue_screen) {
                            extern void screen_queue_set_selected_index(lv_obj_t *screen_obj, int index);
                            screen_queue_set_selected_index(queue_screen, 1);
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    Serial.printf("   → Playing selected recording (index %d)\n", selected_index);
                    g_auto_advance_playback = false;  // Single file playback
                    g_current_playback_index = selected_index;
                }
            }
            
            if (filename.length() == 0) {
                filename = queue_get_oldest_filename();
            }
            
            if (filename.length() > 0) {
                Serial.printf("   → File selected: %s\n", filename.c_str());
                Serial.printf("   → Attempting inline playback (stay in QUEUED)...\n");
                
                // Show progress bar
                if (queue_screen) {
                    screen_queue_show_progress(queue_screen, true);
                    screen_queue_update_progress(queue_screen, 0);  // Start at 0%
                }
                
                // Use 100% volume for queue playback (louder for easier listening)
                audio_set_volume(100);
                Serial.println("   🔊 Queue playback: Using 100% volume");
                
                // Start playback WITHOUT changing state (inline preview)
                if (audio_play_file(filename.c_str())) {
