static const char *TAG = "ES8311";

static inline esp_err_t es8311_write_reg(es8311_handle_t dev, uint8_t reg_addr, uint8_t data)
{
    es8311_dev_t *es = (es8311_dev_t *) dev;
    const uint8_t write_buf[2] = {reg_addr, data};
    return i2cWrite(es->port, es->dev_addr, write_buf, sizeof(write_buf), 1000);
}

static inline esp_err_t es8311_read_reg(es8311_handle_t dev, uint8_t reg_addr, uint8_t *reg_value)
{
    es8311_dev_t *es = (es8311_dev_t *) dev;
    size_t readCount = 0;
    return i2cWriteReadNonStop(es->port, es->dev_addr, &reg_addr, 1, reg_value, 1, 1000, &readCount);
}

/*
* look for the coefficient in coeff_div[] table
*/
static int get_coeff(uint32_t mclk, uint32_t rate)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < (sizeof(coeff_div) / sizeof(coeff_div[0])); i++) {
        if (coeff_div[i].rate == rate && coeff_div[i].mclk == mclk) {
            return i;
        }
    }

    return -1;
}

esp_err_t es8311_sample_frequency_config(es8311_handle_t dev, int mclk_frequency, int sample_frequency)
{
    uint8_t regv;

    /* Get clock coefficients from coefficient table */
    int coeff = get_coeff(mclk_frequency, sample_frequency);

    if (coeff < 0) {
        ESP_LOGE(TAG, "Unable to configure sample rate %dHz with %dHz MCLK", sample_frequency, mclk_frequency);
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    }

    const struct _coeff_div *const selected_coeff = &coeff_div[coeff];

    /* register 0x02 */
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG02, &regv), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    regv &= 0x07;
    regv |= (selected_coeff->pre_div - 1) << 5;
    regv |= selected_coeff->pre_multi << 3;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG02, regv), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    /* register 0x03 */
    const uint8_t reg03 = (selected_coeff->fs_mode << 6) | selected_coeff->adc_osr;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG03, reg03), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    /* register 0x04 */
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG04, selected_coeff->dac_osr), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    /* register 0x05 */
    const uint8_t reg05 = ((selected_coeff->adc_div - 1) << 4) | (selected_coeff->dac_div - 1);
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG05, reg05), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    /* register 0x06 */
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG06, &regv), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    regv &= 0xE0;

    if (selected_coeff->bclk_div < 19) {
        regv |= (selected_coeff->bclk_div - 1) << 0;
    } else {
        regv |= (selected_coeff->bclk_div) << 0;
    }

    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG06, regv), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    /* register 0x07 */
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG07, &regv), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    regv &= 0xC0;
    regv |= selected_coeff->lrck_h << 0;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG07, regv), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    /* register 0x08 */
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG08, selected_coeff->lrck_l), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    return ESP_OK;
}

static esp_err_t es8311_clock_config(es8311_handle_t dev, const es8311_clock_config_t *const clk_cfg, es8311_resolution_t res)
{
    uint8_t reg06;
    uint8_t reg01 = 0x3F; // Enable all clocks
    int mclk_hz;

    /* Select clock source for internal MCLK and determine its frequency */
    if (clk_cfg->mclk_from_mclk_pin) {
        mclk_hz = clk_cfg->mclk_frequency;
    } else {
        mclk_hz = clk_cfg->sample_frequency * (int)res * 2;
        reg01 |= BIT(7); // Select BCLK (a.k.a. SCK) pin
    }

    if (clk_cfg->mclk_inverted) {
        reg01 |= BIT(6); // Invert MCLK pin
    }
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG01, reg01), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG06, &reg06), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    if (clk_cfg->sclk_inverted) {
        reg06 |= BIT(5);
    } else {
        reg06 &= ~BIT(5);
    }
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG06, reg06), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    /* Configure clock dividers */
    return es8311_sample_frequency_config(dev, mclk_hz, clk_cfg->sample_frequency);
}

static esp_err_t es8311_resolution_config(const es8311_resolution_t res, uint8_t *reg)
{
    switch (res) {
    case ES8311_RESOLUTION_16:
        *reg |= (3 << 2);
        break;
    case ES8311_RESOLUTION_18:
        *reg |= (2 << 2);
        break;
    case ES8311_RESOLUTION_20:
        *reg |= (1 << 2);
        break;
    case ES8311_RESOLUTION_24:
        *reg |= (0 << 2);
        break;
    case ES8311_RESOLUTION_32:
        *reg |= (4 << 2);
        break;
    default:
        return ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG;
    }
    return ESP_OK;
}

static esp_err_t es8311_fmt_config(es8311_handle_t dev, const es8311_resolution_t res_in, const es8311_resolution_t res_out)
{
    uint8_t reg09 = 0; // SDP In
    uint8_t reg0a = 0; // SDP Out

    ESP_LOGI(TAG, "ES8311 in Slave mode and I2S format");
    uint8_t reg00;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_RESET_REG00, &reg00), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    reg00 &= 0xBF;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_RESET_REG00, reg00), TAG, "I2C read/write error"); // Slave serial port - default

    /* Setup SDP In and Out resolution */
    es8311_resolution_config(res_in, &reg09);
    es8311_resolution_config(res_out, &reg0a);

    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_SDPIN_REG09, reg09), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_SDPOUT_REG0A, reg0a), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t es8311_microphone_config(es8311_handle_t dev, bool digital_mic)
{
    uint8_t reg14 = 0x1A; // enable analog MIC and max PGA gain

    /* PDM digital microphone enable or disable */
    if (digital_mic) {
        reg14 |= BIT(6);
    }
    es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_ADC_REG17, 0xC8); // Set ADC gain @todo move this to ADC config section

    return es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_SYSTEM_REG14, reg14);
}

esp_err_t es8311_init(es8311_handle_t dev, const es8311_clock_config_t *const clk_cfg, const es8311_resolution_t res_in, const es8311_resolution_t res_out)
{
    ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(
        (clk_cfg->sample_frequency >= 8000) && (clk_cfg->sample_frequency <= 96000),
        ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG, TAG, "ES8311 init needs frequency in interval [8000; 96000] Hz"
    );
    if (!clk_cfg->mclk_from_mclk_pin) {
        ESP_RETURN_ON_FALSE(res_out == res_in, ESP_ERR_INVALID_ARG, TAG, "Resolution IN/OUT must be equal if MCLK is taken from SCK pin");
    }


    /* Reset ES8311 to its default */
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_RESET_REG00, 0x1F), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    vTaskDelay(20);
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_RESET_REG00, 0x00), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_RESET_REG00, 0x80), TAG, "I2C read/write error"); // Power-on command

    /* Setup clock: source, polarity and clock dividers */
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_clock_config(dev, clk_cfg, res_out), TAG, "");

    /* Setup audio format (fmt): master/slave, resolution, I2S */
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_fmt_config(dev, res_in, res_out), TAG, "");

    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_SYSTEM_REG0D, 0x01), TAG, "I2C read/write error"); // Power up analog circuitry - NOT default
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_SYSTEM_REG0E, 0x02), TAG, "I2C read/write error"); // Enable analog PGA, enable ADC modulator - NOT default
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_SYSTEM_REG12, 0x00), TAG, "I2C read/write error"); // power-up DAC - NOT default
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_SYSTEM_REG13, 0x10), TAG, "I2C read/write error"); // Enable output to HP drive - NOT default
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_ADC_REG1C, 0x6A), TAG, "I2C read/write error"); // ADC Equalizer bypass, cancel DC offset in digital domain
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_DAC_REG37, 0x08), TAG, "I2C read/write error"); // Bypass DAC equalizer - NOT default

    return ESP_OK;
}

void es8311_delete(es8311_handle_t dev)
{
    free(dev);
}

esp_err_t es8311_voice_volume_set(es8311_handle_t dev, int volume, int *volume_set)
{
    if (volume < 0) {
        volume = 0;
    } else if (volume > 100) {
        volume = 100;
    }

    int reg32;
    if (volume == 0) {
        reg32 = 0;
    } else {
        reg32 = ((volume) * 256 / 100) - 1;
    }

    // provide user with real volume set
    if (volume_set != NULL) {
        *volume_set = volume;
    }
    return es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_DAC_REG32, reg32);
}

esp_err_t es8311_voice_volume_get(es8311_handle_t dev, int *volume)
{
    uint8_t reg32;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_DAC_REG32, &reg32), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    if (reg32 == 0) {
        *volume = 0;
    } else {
        *volume = ((reg32 * 100) / 256) + 1;
    }
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t es8311_voice_mute(es8311_handle_t dev, bool mute)
{
    uint8_t reg31;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_DAC_REG31, &reg31), TAG, "I2C read/write error");

    if (mute) {
        reg31 |= BIT(6) | BIT(5);
    } else {
        reg31 &= ~(BIT(6) | BIT(5));
    }

    return es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_DAC_REG31, reg31);
}

esp_err_t es8311_microphone_gain_set(es8311_handle_t dev, es8311_mic_gain_t gain_db)
{
    return es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_ADC_REG16, gain_db); // ADC gain scale up
}

esp_err_t es8311_voice_fade(es8311_handle_t dev, const es8311_fade_t fade)
{
    uint8_t reg37;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_DAC_REG37, &reg37), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    reg37 &= 0x0F;
    reg37 |= (fade << 4);
    return es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_DAC_REG37, reg37);
}

esp_err_t es8311_microphone_fade(es8311_handle_t dev, const es8311_fade_t fade)
{
    uint8_t reg15;
    ESP_RETURN_ON_ERROR(es8311_read_reg(dev, ES8311_ADC_REG15, &reg15), TAG, "I2C read/write error");
    reg15 &= 0x0F;
    reg15 |= (fade << 4);
    return es8311_write_reg(dev, ES8311_ADC_REG15, reg15);
}

void es8311_register_dump(es8311_handle_t dev)
{
    for (int reg = 0; reg < 0x4A; reg++) {
        uint8_t value;
        ESP_ERROR_CHECK(es8311_read_reg(dev, reg, &value));
        printf("REG:%02x: %02x", reg, value);
    }
}

es8311_handle_t es8311_create(const unsigned int port, const uint16_t dev_addr)
{
    es8311_dev_t *sensor = (es8311_dev_t *) calloc(1, sizeof(es8311_dev_t));
    sensor->port = port;
    sensor->dev_addr = dev_addr;
    return (es8311_handle_t) sensor;
}

// ============================================
// ES8311 CODEC DRIVER - Implementation
// ============================================
// Wrapper around ES8311 codec functions

#include "es8311_driver.h"
#include "audio_pins.h"

// Include the actual ES8311 driver
// These files need to be copied from Platformio_audio_test/src or Device_Rom_Complete
extern "C" {
    #include "es8311.h"
}

// Configuration constants
#define EXAMPLE_SAMPLE_RATE 16000
#define EXAMPLE_VOICE_VOLUME 80
#define EXAMPLE_MIC_GAIN 4

// Global ES8311 codec configuration
static es8311_handle_t es_handle = NULL;

esp_err_t es8311_codec_init() {
    Serial.println("🎙️ Initializing ES8311 codec...");
    
    // Create ES8311 handle
    es_handle = es8311_create(I2C_NUM_0, ES8311_I2C_ADDRESS);
    if (es_handle == NULL) {
        Serial.println("❌ Failed to create ES8311 handle");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    
    // Configure clock
    es8311_clock_config_t clk_cfg = {
        .mclk_inverted = false,
        .sclk_inverted = false,
        .mclk_from_mclk_pin = true,
        .mclk_frequency = EXAMPLE_SAMPLE_RATE * 256,
        .sample_frequency = EXAMPLE_SAMPLE_RATE
    };
    
    // Initialize codec
    if (es8311_init(es_handle, &clk_cfg, ES8311_RESOLUTION_16, ES8311_RESOLUTION_16) != ESP_OK) {
        Serial.println("❌ ES8311 init failed");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    
    // Configure sample frequency
    if (es8311_sample_frequency_config(es_handle, clk_cfg.mclk_frequency, clk_cfg.sample_frequency) != ESP_OK) {
        Serial.println("❌ ES8311 sample frequency config failed");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    
    // Configure microphone
    if (es8311_microphone_config(es_handle, false) != ESP_OK) {
        Serial.println("❌ ES8311 microphone config failed");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    
    // Set volume
    if (es8311_voice_volume_set(es_handle, EXAMPLE_VOICE_VOLUME, NULL) != ESP_OK) {
        Serial.println("❌ ES8311 volume set failed");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    
    // Set mic gain
    if (es8311_microphone_gain_set(es_handle, (es8311_mic_gain_t)EXAMPLE_MIC_GAIN) != ESP_OK) {
        Serial.println("❌ ES8311 mic gain set failed");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    
    // Unmute
    if (es8311_voice_mute(es_handle, false) != ESP_OK) {
        Serial.println("❌ ES8311 unmute failed");
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    
    Serial.println("✅ ES8311 codec initialized");
    return ESP_OK;
}

esp_err_t es8311_codec_set_voice_volume(uint8_t volume) {
    if (es_handle == NULL) {
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    return es8311_voice_volume_set(es_handle, volume, NULL);
}

esp_err_t es8311_codec_set_mic_gain(uint8_t gain) {
    if (es_handle == NULL) {
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    return es8311_microphone_gain_set(es_handle, (es8311_mic_gain_t)gain);
}

esp_err_t es8311_codec_set_voice_mute(bool mute) {
    if (es_handle == NULL) {
        return ESP_FAIL;
    }
    return es8311_voice_mute(es_handle, mute);
}

// AUDIO MANAGER - ES8311 + Legacy I2S Driver
// ============================================
// Modular audio system using proven working pattern:
// - Arduino Wire.begin() for I2C
// - Legacy ESP-IDF I2S driver (i2s_driver_install)
// - ES8311 codec driver
// - Recording to SD card with streaming
// - Playback from SD card

#ifndef AUDIO_MANAGER_H
#define AUDIO_MANAGER_H

#include <Arduino.h>

// Audio configuration
#define AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE 16000
#define AUDIO_BITS_PER_SAMPLE 16
#define AUDIO_CHANNELS 1  // Mono
#define AUDIO_VOICE_VOLUME 80  // 0-100
#define AUDIO_MIC_GAIN 4  // 0-7 (4 = good balance, avoid clipping)

#define AUDIO_RECORD_BUFFER_SIZE (2 * 1024 * 1024)  // 2MB circular buffer (we have 8MB PSRAM!)
#define AUDIO_MAX_RECORD_TIME_MS 60000  // 60 seconds max
#define AUDIO_STREAM_CHUNK_SIZE (64 * 1024)  // Write to SD every 64KB (larger chunks for efficiency)

 * @file
 * @brief ES8311 driver

#include "esp_types.h"
#include "esp_err.h"

/* ES8311 address: CE pin low - 0x18, CE pin high - 0x19 */
#define ES8311_ADDRRES_0 0x18u // Leaving this here for backward compatibility
#define ES8311_ADDRESS_0 0x18u
#define ES8311_ADDRESS_1 0x19u

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef void *es8311_handle_t;

typedef enum {
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_MIN = -1,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_0DB,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_6DB,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_12DB,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_18DB,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_24DB,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_30DB,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_36DB,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_42DB,
    ES8311_MIC_GAIN_MAX
} es8311_mic_gain_t;

typedef enum {
    ES8311_FADE_OFF = 0,
    ES8311_FADE_4LRCK, // 4LRCK means ramp 0.25dB/4LRCK
    ES8311_FADE_8LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_16LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_32LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_64LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_128LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_256LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_512LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_1024LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_2048LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_4096LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_8192LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_16384LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_32768LRCK,
    ES8311_FADE_65536LRCK
} es8311_fade_t;

typedef enum es8311_resolution_t {
    ES8311_RESOLUTION_16 = 16,
    ES8311_RESOLUTION_18 = 18,
    ES8311_RESOLUTION_20 = 20,
    ES8311_RESOLUTION_24 = 24,
    ES8311_RESOLUTION_32 = 32
} es8311_resolution_t;

typedef struct es8311_clock_config_t {
    bool mclk_inverted;
    bool sclk_inverted;
    bool mclk_from_mclk_pin; // true: from MCLK pin (pin no. 2), false: from SCLK pin (pin no. 6)
    int  mclk_frequency;     // This parameter is ignored if MCLK is taken from SCLK pin
    int  sample_frequency;   // in Hz
} es8311_clock_config_t;

esp_err_t es8311_init(es8311_handle_t dev, const es8311_clock_config_t *const clk_cfg, const es8311_resolution_t res_in,
                      const es8311_resolution_t res_out);

esp_err_t es8311_voice_volume_get(es8311_handle_t dev, int *volume);

esp_err_t es8311_sample_frequency_config(es8311_handle_t dev, int mclk_frequency, int sample_frequency);
esp_err_t es8311_voice_fade(es8311_handle_t dev, const es8311_fade_t fade);
esp_err_t es8311_microphone_fade(es8311_handle_t dev, const es8311_fade_t fade);
es8311_handle_t es8311_create(const unsigned int port, const uint16_t dev_addr);
void es8311_delete(es8311_handle_t dev);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

/*

#pragma once

/*
 *   ES8311_REGISTER NAME_REG_REGISTER ADDRESS
 */
#define ES8311_RESET_REG00              0x00  /*reset digital,csm,clock manager etc.*/

/*
 * Clock Scheme Register definition
 */
#define ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG01        0x01 /* select clk src for mclk, enable clock for codec */
#define ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG02        0x02 /* clk divider and clk multiplier */
#define ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG03        0x03 /* adc fsmode and osr  */
#define ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG04        0x04 /* dac osr */
#define ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG05        0x05 /* clk divier for adc and dac */
#define ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG06        0x06 /* bclk inverter and divider */
#define ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG07        0x07 /* tri-state, lrck divider */
#define ES8311_CLK_MANAGER_REG08        0x08 /* lrck divider */
/*
 * SDP
 */
#define ES8311_SDPIN_REG09              0x09 /* dac serial digital port */
#define ES8311_SDPOUT_REG0A             0x0A /* adc serial digital port */
/*
 * SYSTEM
 */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG0B             0x0B /* system */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG0C             0x0C /* system */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG0D             0x0D /* system, power up/down */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG0E             0x0E /* system, power up/down */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG0F             0x0F /* system, low power */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG10             0x10 /* system */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG11             0x11 /* system */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG12             0x12 /* system, Enable DAC */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG13             0x13 /* system */
#define ES8311_SYSTEM_REG14             0x14 /* system, select DMIC, select analog pga gain */
/*
 * ADC
 */
#define ES8311_ADC_REG15                0x15 /* ADC, adc ramp rate, dmic sense */
#define ES8311_ADC_REG16                0x16 /* ADC */
#define ES8311_ADC_REG17                0x17 /* ADC, volume */
#define ES8311_ADC_REG18                0x18 /* ADC, alc enable and winsize */
#define ES8311_ADC_REG19                0x19 /* ADC, alc maxlevel */
#define ES8311_ADC_REG1A                0x1A /* ADC, alc automute */
#define ES8311_ADC_REG1B                0x1B /* ADC, alc automute, adc hpf s1 */
#define ES8311_ADC_REG1C                0x1C /* ADC, equalizer, hpf s2 */
/*
 * DAC
 */
#define ES8311_DAC_REG31                0x31 /* DAC, mute */
#define ES8311_DAC_REG32                0x32 /* DAC, volume */
#define ES8311_DAC_REG33                0x33 /* DAC, offset */
#define ES8311_DAC_REG34                0x34 /* DAC, drc enable, drc winsize */
#define ES8311_DAC_REG35                0x35 /* DAC, drc maxlevel, minilevel */
#define ES8311_DAC_REG37                0x37 /* DAC, ramprate */
/*
 *GPIO
 */

#define ES8311_GP_REG45                 0x45 /* GP CONTROL */
/*
 * CHIP

#define ES8311_CHD2_REGFE               0xFE /* CHIP ID2 */
#define ES8311_CHVER_REGFF              0xFF /* VERSION */

[display_galaxy.cpp - C:\ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-1.8\ESP-IDF-v5.3.2\LVGL_Native_PlatformIO\src\display\display_galaxy.cpp] 
============================================ 
/**
 * @file display_galaxy.cpp
 * @brief 3D Galaxy animation - DIRECT PIXEL UPDATES for IDLE screen
 * 
 * Bypasses LVGL for star rendering - writes pixels directly to display.
 * Only updates changed pixels (erase old + draw new) = ~400 pixels/frame instead of 330KB!
 * 
 * Features:
 * - 500 stars in 3D elliptical orbits
 * - IMU-based parallax effect (tilting device shifts galaxy)
 * - Direct pixel rendering for blazing performance
 * - Automatically destroyed when leaving IDLE state
 */

#include "display/display_galaxy.h"
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <Arduino_GFX_Library.h>
#include <math.h>

// Configuration
#ifndef GALAXY_N
#define GALAXY_N 500  // 500 stars - direct pixel updates are blazing fast!
#endif

// Star structure - 3D elliptical orbit with position tracking
typedef struct {
    // Orbit parameters
    float orbit_a;    // ellipse semi-major axis
    float orbit_b;    // ellipse semi-minor axis
    float cos_tilt;   // pre-calculated cos(tilt)
    float sin_tilt;   // pre-calculated sin(tilt)
    float angle;      // current angle on orbit (radians)
    float speed;      // angular velocity (radians/frame)
    
    // Visual properties
    uint16_t base_color;  // Star base color (white, yellowish, blueish)
    float brightness;     // Brightness phase (0-2π for pulsing)
    float brightness_speed; // How fast brightness oscillates
    
    // Position tracking for direct pixel updates
    int16_t x, y;         // Current position
    int16_t prev_x, prev_y;  // Previous position (for erasing)
    bool visible;     // Is this star currently visible?
} star_t;

// Galaxy state
typedef struct {
    star_t s[GALAXY_N];    // Stars array
    int w, h;              // Widget dimensions
    int cx, cy;            // Center coordinates
    float radius_px;       // Orbit radius
    int visible_count;     // Number of visible stars (always GALAXY_N for IDLE)
    lv_timer_t *timer;     // Animation timer
    
    // IMU-based 3D parallax effect
    float imu_offset_x;          // Parallax X offset in pixels
    float imu_offset_y;          // Parallax Y offset in pixels
    float imu_smoothed_x;        // Smoothed offset X (for damping)
    float imu_smoothed_y;        // Smoothed offset Y (for damping)
} galaxy_t;

// Global galaxy instance (needed for direct updates)
static galaxy_t *g_galaxy = nullptr;

/**
 * @brief Update star physics and positions
 */
static void galaxy_update_positions(galaxy_t *g) {
    for (int i = 0; i < g->visible_count; i++) {
        star_t *s = &g->s[i];
        
        // Save previous position
        s->prev_x = s->x;
        s->prev_y = s->y;
        
        // Advance angle
        s->angle += s->speed;
        if (s->angle > 6.2832f) s->angle -= 6.2832f;
        
        // Advance brightness phase
        if (s->brightness_speed > 0.0f) {
            s->brightness += s->brightness_speed;
            if (s->brightness > 6.2832f) s->brightness -= 6.2832f;
        }
        
        // Calculate 3D position
        float cos_angle = cosf(s->angle);
        float sin_angle = sinf(s->angle);
        
        float orbit_x = s->orbit_a * cos_angle;
        float orbit_y = s->orbit_b * sin_angle;
        
        float x3d = orbit_x * s->cos_tilt - orbit_y * s->sin_tilt;
        float y3d = orbit_x * s->sin_tilt + orbit_y * s->cos_tilt;
        
        // Apply global IMU-based parallax offset (same for ALL stars - no depth scaling)
        // This creates a simple panning effect when device is tilted
        float parallax_x = g->imu_smoothed_x;
        float parallax_y = g->imu_smoothed_y;
        
        // Project to screen with parallax
        s->x = g->cx + (int16_t)(x3d * g->radius_px + parallax_x);
        s->y = g->cy + (int16_t)(y3d * g->radius_px + parallax_y);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Apply brightness modulation to base color
 */
static uint16_t apply_brightness(uint16_t base_color, float brightness_phase) {
    // Calculate brightness (0.2 to 1.0 range for MORE dramatic pulsing)
    float brightness = 0.2f + 0.8f * (1.0f + cosf(brightness_phase)) / 2.0f;
    
    // Extract RGB565 components
    uint8_t r = (base_color >> 11) & 0x1F;
    uint8_t g = (base_color >> 5) & 0x3F;
    uint8_t b = base_color & 0x1F;
    
    // Apply brightness
    r = (uint8_t)(r * brightness);
    g = (uint8_t)(g * brightness);
    b = (uint8_t)(b * brightness);
    
    // Repack RGB565
    return (r << 11) | (g << 5) | b;
}

/**
 * @brief Check if position is in UI exclusion zone
 */
static bool is_in_ui_zone(int16_t x, int16_t y, int w, int h) {
    // Top left (battery): x < 80, y < 30
    if (x < 80 && y < 30) return true;
    
    // Top right (WiFi): reduced by 30px from left -> x > w-50, y < 30
    if (x > w - 50 && y < 30) return true;
    
    // Top right (settings button): x > w-30, y between 60-85
    if (x > w - 30 && y > 60 && y < 85) return true;
    
    // Top right (mic button - moved down): x > w-30, y between 89-114
    if (x > w - 30 && y > 89 && y < 114) return true;
    
    // Bottom left - "IDLE" text only: x < 100, y > h-80, y < h-45
    if (x < 100 && y > h - 80 && y < h - 45) return true;
    
    // Bottom left - "Ready for command" text: x < 193, y > h-45, y < h-15 (extended 3px right)
    if (x < 193 && y > h - 45 && y < h - 15) return true;
    
    // Bottom right (power button - moved up 5px, right 5px): x > w-30, y > h-85, y < h-55
    if (x > w - 30 && y > h - 85 && y < h - 55) return true;
    
    return false;
}

/**
 * @brief Render stars via direct pixel updates (called after LVGL flush)
 */
void galaxy_render_direct(Arduino_GFX *gfx) {
    if (!g_galaxy) return;
    
    
    gfx->startWrite();  // Begin direct access
    
    for (int i = 0; i < g_galaxy->visible_count; i++) {
        star_t *s = &g_galaxy->s[i];
        
        // Erase old position (only if it moved and not in UI zone)
        if (s->prev_x != s->x || s->prev_y != s->y) {
            if (s->prev_x >= 0 && s->prev_x < g_galaxy->w && 
                s->prev_y >= 0 && s->prev_y < g_galaxy->h) {
                if (!is_in_ui_zone(s->prev_x, s->prev_y, g_galaxy->w, g_galaxy->h)) {
                    gfx->writePixel(s->prev_x, s->prev_y, 0x0000);  // Erase to black
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Draw new position (only if not in UI zone)
        if (s->x >= 0 && s->x < g_galaxy->w && 
            s->y >= 0 && s->y < g_galaxy->h) {
            if (!is_in_ui_zone(s->x, s->y, g_galaxy->w, g_galaxy->h)) {
                uint16_t color = (s->brightness_speed > 0.0f) 
                    ? apply_brightness(s->base_color, s->brightness)
                    : s->base_color;
                gfx->writePixel(s->x, s->y, color);
            }
        }
    }
    
    gfx->endWrite();
}


/**
 * @brief Timer callback - update physics only
 */
static void galaxy_timer_cb(lv_timer_t *t) {
    galaxy_t *g = (galaxy_t*)t->user_data;
    if (!g) return;
    
    galaxy_update_positions(g);
    // Note: Actual rendering happens in galaxy_render_direct() after LVGL flush
}

/**
 * @brief Delete callback (cleanup)
 */
static void galaxy_del_cb(lv_event_t *e) {
    galaxy_t *g = (galaxy_t*)lv_event_get_user_data(e);
    if (g) {
        if (g->timer) lv_timer_del(g->timer);
        if (g == g_galaxy) g_galaxy = nullptr;
        lv_mem_free(g);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Create galaxy widget (public API)
 */
lv_obj_t* galaxy_create(lv_obj_t *parent, int w, int h, uint32_t fps) {
    // Create invisible container (just for lifecycle management)
    lv_obj_t *container = lv_obj_create(parent);
    lv_obj_add_flag(container, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);  // Invisible - we render directly
    lv_obj_set_size(container, w, h);
    
    // Allocate galaxy state
    galaxy_t *g = (galaxy_t*)lv_mem_alloc(sizeof(galaxy_t));
    LV_ASSERT_MALLOC(g);
    memset(g, 0, sizeof(galaxy_t));
    
    g->w = w;
    g->h = h;
    g->cx = w / 2;
    g->cy = (h / 2) - 10;  // Render 10px higher
    g->radius_px = (LV_MIN(w, h) * 0.55f);  // Larger radius so some orbits extend beyond screen
    g->visible_count = GALAXY_N;  // All 500 stars visible
    
    // Initialize IMU parallax offset
    g->imu_offset_x = 0.0f;
    g->imu_offset_y = 0.0f;
    g->imu_smoothed_x = 0.0f;
    g->imu_smoothed_y = 0.0f;
    
    // Initialize stars with random orbits
    for (int i = 0; i < GALAXY_N; i++) {
        star_t *s = &g->s[i];
        
        // Orbit size: 0.1 to 1.3 (some beyond screen!)
        float dist = 0.1f + (lv_rand(0, 1200)) / 1000.0f;
        
        // More elliptical orbits - 70% get high eccentricity (0-0.9)
        float eccentricity;
        if (lv_rand(0, 100) < 70) {
            // 70% highly elliptical
            eccentricity = 0.4f + (lv_rand(0, 500)) / 1000.0f;  // 0.4-0.9
        } else {
            // 30% more circular
            eccentricity = (lv_rand(0, 400)) / 1000.0f;  // 0-0.4
        }
        s->orbit_a = dist;
        s->orbit_b = dist * (1.0f - eccentricity * 0.85f);
        
        // 3D tilt
        float tilt = (lv_rand(0, 3600)) / 572.96f;
        s->cos_tilt = cosf(tilt);
        s->sin_tilt = sinf(tilt);
        
        // Random starting angle
        s->angle = (lv_rand(0, 3600)) / 572.96f;
        
        // Variable speed based on distance (Kepler's laws - inner orbits faster!)
        // Base speed inversely proportional to distance
        float base_speed = 0.012f / (dist + 0.2f);  // Closer = faster
        s->speed = base_speed + (lv_rand(0, 100)) / 50000.0f;  // Add variation
        
        // Star color variety (RGB565 format) - more blue, with red and purple!
        int color_type = lv_rand(0, 100);
        if (color_type < 40) {
            // 40% Pure white
            s->base_color = 0xFFFF;
        } else if (color_type < 65) {
            // 25% Cool blue-white
            s->base_color = 0xBDFF;  // RGB(184, 223, 255)
        } else if (color_type < 78) {
            // 13% Warm yellow-white
            s->base_color = 0xFFF0;  // RGB(255, 254, 0)
        } else if (color_type < 86) {
            // 8% Orange-white
            s->base_color = 0xFE60;  // RGB(255, 204, 0)
        } else if (color_type < 96) {
            // 10% Pale gray-white
            s->base_color = 0xCE59;  // RGB(200, 200, 200)
        } else if (color_type < 99) {
            // 3% Purple-white (much whiter, subtle purple tint)
            s->base_color = 0xFDFF;  // RGB(248, 224, 255) very pale purple
        } else {
            // 1% RED stars! (~5 stars, much whiter with red tint)
            s->base_color = 0xFDF0;  // RGB(248, 224, 192) pale red-orange
        }
        
        // More stars pulse - 60% instead of 40%
        s->brightness = (lv_rand(0, 628)) / 100.0f;  // Random phase 0-2π
        s->brightness_speed = (lv_rand(0, 100) < 60) ? (lv_rand(5, 25) / 10000.0f) : 0.0f;  // 60% pulse
        
        s->x = s->y = -1;  // Off-screen initially
        s->prev_x = s->prev_y = -1;
        s->visible = (i < g->visible_count);
    }
    
    // Do initial position calculation
    galaxy_update_positions(g);
    
    // Set as global instance
    g_galaxy = g;
    
    lv_obj_set_user_data(container, g);
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(container, galaxy_del_cb, LV_EVENT_DELETE, g);
    
    // Create animation timer (30ms = ~30 FPS physics)
    uint32_t period = LV_MAX(16, 1000 / LV_CLAMP(1, fps, 60));
    g->timer = lv_timer_create(galaxy_timer_cb, period, g);
    
    return container;
}

/**
 * @brief Set GFX instance for direct rendering (no-op, kept for API compatibility)
 */
void galaxy_set_gfx(Arduino_GFX *gfx) {
    // No-op: gfx is passed directly to galaxy_render_direct()
    (void)gfx;
}


/**
 * @brief Set IMU-based 3D parallax offset
 * 
 * Creates a subtle 3D depth effect where the galaxy shifts based on device tilt.
 * The effect simulates looking through a window into deep space - when you tilt
 * the device, the galaxy shifts in the opposite direction (like parallax).
 * 
 * Far stars (smaller orbits) move less than near stars (larger orbits),
 * creating a convincing depth effect.
 */
void galaxy_set_imu_offset(float accel_x, float accel_y, float accel_z) {
    if (!g_galaxy) return;
    
    // Configuration
    const float MAX_OFFSET = 20.0f;      // Maximum parallax offset in pixels (subtle effect)
    const float SMOOTHING = 0.20f;        // Smoothing factor (0.0-1.0, lower = smoother)
    const float DEADZONE = 0.02f;         // Ignore small movements (noise reduction)
    
    // Calculate tilt angles from accelerometer
    // Correct axis mapping for natural parallax:
    // - Tilt right (positive accel_x) → stars shift left (negative X offset)
    // - Tilt down (positive accel_y) → stars shift down (positive Y offset)
    // Note: Axes were swapped - accel_y was controlling X, accel_x was controlling Y
    float tilt_x = -accel_y;  // Y accel controls horizontal shift (inverted)
    float tilt_y = accel_x;   // X accel controls vertical shift (not inverted)
    
    // Apply deadzone to prevent jitter
    if (fabsf(tilt_x) < DEADZONE) tilt_x = 0.0f;
    if (fabsf(tilt_y) < DEADZONE) tilt_y = 0.0f;
    
    // Clamp to reasonable tilt range (±1.0g)
    tilt_x = LV_CLAMP(-1.0f, tilt_x, 1.0f);
    tilt_y = LV_CLAMP(-1.0f, tilt_y, 1.0f);
    
    // Calculate target offset (linear mapping: ±1.0g → ±MAX_OFFSET pixels)
    float target_x = tilt_x * MAX_OFFSET;
    float target_y = tilt_y * MAX_OFFSET;
    
    // Smooth the offset using exponential moving average
    // This prevents jittery motion and makes the effect feel more natural
    g_galaxy->imu_smoothed_x += (target_x - g_galaxy->imu_smoothed_x) * SMOOTHING;
    g_galaxy->imu_smoothed_y += (target_y - g_galaxy->imu_smoothed_y) * SMOOTHING;
    
    
    // The actual parallax is applied in galaxy_update_positions()
    // where each star's offset is scaled by its depth_factor
}

/**
 * @file display_icons.cpp
 * @brief Custom icon drawing implementation
 * 
 * Canvas-based icon rendering inspired by Device_Rom_Complete project
 */

#include "display_icons.h"
#include <stdlib.h>

// ============================================
// SMALL MIC ICON (20x20px) - For button indicators
// ============================================
/**
 * Original geometry from Device_Rom_Complete:
 * - Circle at (x, y-5), radius 8
 * - Rect from (x-8, y-5) to (x+8, y+7)  → 16w × 12h
 * - Stem from (x-2, y+7) to (x+2, y+15) → 4w × 8h
 * - Base from (x-6, y+15) to (x+6, y+18) → 12w × 3h
 * 
 * Scaled for 20x20 canvas with center at (10, 10):
 */
lv_obj_t* icon_create_mic_small(lv_obj_t *parent, lv_color_t color) {
    // Create canvas (20x20 pixels, ARGB8888 for transparency)
    lv_obj_t *canvas = lv_canvas_create(parent);
    lv_obj_set_size(canvas, 20, 20);
    
    // Allocate buffer (20 × 20 × 4 bytes = 1600 bytes)
    static lv_color_t cbuf[20 * 20];
    lv_canvas_set_buffer(canvas, cbuf, 20, 20, LV_IMG_CF_TRUE_COLOR);
    
    // Clear to transparent
    lv_canvas_fill_bg(canvas, lv_color_hex(0x000000), LV_OPA_TRANSP);
    
    // Set draw color
    lv_draw_rect_dsc_t rect_dsc;
    lv_draw_rect_dsc_init(&rect_dsc);
    rect_dsc.bg_color = color;
    rect_dsc.bg_opa = LV_OPA_COVER;
    rect_dsc.border_width = 0;
    
    // Mic capsule top (circle)
    rect_dsc.radius = 8;
    lv_canvas_draw_rect(canvas, 2, 0, 16, 16, &rect_dsc);
    
    // Mic capsule body (rounded rectangle)
    rect_dsc.radius = 0;
    lv_canvas_draw_rect(canvas, 2, 6, 16, 10, &rect_dsc);
    
    // Mic stem (vertical bar)
    lv_canvas_draw_rect(canvas, 8, 12, 4, 6, &rect_dsc);
    
    // Mic base (horizontal bar)
    lv_canvas_draw_rect(canvas, 4, 18, 12, 2, &rect_dsc);
    
    return canvas;
}

// ============================================
// LARGE MIC ICON (80x100px) - For center of screen
// ============================================
/**
 * Large microphone for recording screen center
 * Scaled 4× from small version
 */
lv_obj_t* icon_create_mic_large(lv_obj_t *parent, lv_color_t color) {
    // Create canvas (80x100 pixels)
    lv_obj_t *canvas = lv_canvas_create(parent);
    lv_obj_set_size(canvas, 80, 100);
    
    // Allocate buffer (80 × 100 × 4 bytes = 32000 bytes = 31.25KB)
    static lv_color_t cbuf_large[80 * 100];
    lv_canvas_set_buffer(canvas, cbuf_large, 80, 100, LV_IMG_CF_TRUE_COLOR);
    
    // Clear to transparent
    lv_canvas_fill_bg(canvas, lv_color_hex(0x000000), LV_OPA_TRANSP);
    
    // Set draw color
    lv_draw_rect_dsc_t rect_dsc;
    lv_draw_rect_dsc_init(&rect_dsc);
    rect_dsc.bg_color = color;
    rect_dsc.bg_opa = LV_OPA_COVER;
    rect_dsc.border_width = 0;
    
    // Mic capsule top (rounded rectangle, acts like circle)
    rect_dsc.radius = 30;
    lv_canvas_draw_rect(canvas, 10, 0, 60, 60, &rect_dsc);
    
    // Mic capsule body (rounded rectangle)
    rect_dsc.radius = 15;
    lv_canvas_draw_rect(canvas, 10, 20, 60, 45, &rect_dsc);
    
    // Mic stem (vertical bar)
    rect_dsc.radius = 0;
    lv_canvas_draw_rect(canvas, 30, 50, 20, 30, &rect_dsc);
    
    // Mic base (horizontal bar)
    lv_canvas_draw_rect(canvas, 16, 80, 48, 8, &rect_dsc);
    
    return canvas;
}

/**
 * @file display_manager.cpp
 * @brief Display manager implementation
 */

#include <Arduino.h>
#include "display/display_manager.h"
#include "display/display_screens.h"
#include <esp_heap_caps.h>

// Manager state
typedef struct {
    lv_obj_t *screen_boot;
    lv_obj_t *screen_idle;
    lv_obj_t *screen_recording;
    lv_obj_t *screen_uploading;
    lv_obj_t *screen_processing;
    lv_obj_t *screen_downloading;
    lv_obj_t *screen_playing;
    lv_obj_t *screen_confirmation;
    lv_obj_t *screen_queued;
    lv_obj_t *screen_sleep;
    lv_obj_t *screen_settings;
    lv_obj_t *screen_error;
    
    display_state_t current_state;
    display_event_cb_t event_cb;
    idle_status_t status;
} display_manager_t;

static display_manager_t manager = {
    .screen_boot = NULL,
    .screen_idle = NULL,
    .screen_recording = NULL,
    .screen_uploading = NULL,
    .screen_processing = NULL,
    .screen_downloading = NULL,
    .screen_playing = NULL,
    .screen_confirmation = NULL,
    .screen_queued = NULL,
    .screen_sleep = NULL,
    .screen_settings = NULL,
    .screen_error = NULL,
    .current_state = DISPLAY_STATE_BOOT,
    .event_cb = NULL,
    .status = {0}
};

/**
 * @brief Log memory usage for debugging
 */
static void log_memory(const char* tag) {
    size_t internal_free = heap_caps_get_free_size(MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL);
    size_t internal_largest = heap_caps_get_largest_free_block(MALLOC_CAP_INTERNAL);
    size_t psram_free = heap_caps_get_free_size(MALLOC_CAP_SPIRAM);
    
    Serial.printf("[MEM][%s] Internal: %u KB free (largest: %u KB) | PSRAM: %u MB free\n",
        tag,
        (unsigned)(internal_free / 1024),
        (unsigned)(internal_largest / 1024),
        (unsigned)(psram_free / 1024 / 1024));
}

/**
 * @brief Delete a screen and set pointer to NULL
 */
static void delete_screen(lv_obj_t** screen_ptr) {
    if (screen_ptr && *screen_ptr) {
        lv_obj_del(*screen_ptr);
        *screen_ptr = NULL;
    }
}

/**
 * @brief IDLE screen event handler
 */
static void idle_event_handler(idle_event_t event) {
    if (!manager.event_cb) return;
    
    switch (event) {
        case IDLE_EVENT_MIC_CLICKED:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 1);  // Event data: 1 = mic
            break;
        case IDLE_EVENT_POWER_CLICKED:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 2);  // Event data: 2 = power
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
}

/**
 * @brief RECORDING screen event handler
 */
static void recording_event_handler(recording_event_t event) {
    if (!manager.event_cb) return;
    
    switch (event) {
        case RECORDING_EVENT_STOP_CLICKED:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 3);  // Event data: 3 = stop
            break;
        case RECORDING_EVENT_POWER_CLICKED:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 2);  // Event data: 2 = power
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
}

/**
 * @brief UPLOADING screen event handler
 */
static void uploading_event_handler(uploading_event_t event) {
    if (!manager.event_cb) return;
    
    switch (event) {
        case UPLOADING_EVENT_CANCEL_CLICKED:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 4);  // Event data: 4 = cancel
            break;
        case UPLOADING_EVENT_POWER_CLICKED:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 2);  // Event data: 2 = power
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Settings screen event handler
 */
static void settings_event_handler(settings_event_data_t event_data) {
    if (!manager.event_cb) return;
    
    // Forward settings events to main app
    // Event data format: event type (0-3) * 100 + value
    int encoded = (int)event_data.event * 100 + event_data.value;
    manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, encoded);
}

/**
 * @brief Queue screen event handler
 */
static void queue_event_handler(queue_event_t event) {
    if (!manager.event_cb) return;
    
    switch (event) {
        case QUEUE_EVENT_PROCESS:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 0);  // Event data: 0 = QUEUE_EVENT_PROCESS
            break;
        case QUEUE_EVENT_DELETE_ALL:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 1);  // Event data: 1 = QUEUE_EVENT_DELETE_ALL
            break;
        case QUEUE_EVENT_PLAY:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 2);  // Event data: 2 = QUEUE_EVENT_PLAY
            break;
        case QUEUE_EVENT_STOP:
            manager.event_cb(manager.current_state, 3);  // Event data: 3 = QUEUE_EVENT_STOP
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Create IDLE screen if not already created
 */
static void ensure_idle_screen(void) {
    if (!manager.screen_idle) {
        manager.screen_idle = screen_idle_create(lv_scr_act(), idle_event_handler);
        screen_idle_update_status(manager.screen_idle, &manager.status);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Create BOOT screen if not already created
 */
static void ensure_boot_screen(void) {
    if (!manager.screen_boot) {
        manager.screen_boot = screen_boot_create(lv_scr_act());
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Create RECORDING screen if not already created
 */
static void ensure_recording_screen(void) {
    if (!manager.screen_recording) {
        manager.screen_recording = screen_recording_create(lv_scr_act(), recording_event_handler);
        screen_recording_update_status(manager.screen_recording, &manager.status);
        screen_recording_update_duration(manager.screen_recording, 0);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Create UPLOADING screen if not already created
 */
static void ensure_uploading_screen(void) {
    if (!manager.screen_uploading) {
        manager.screen_uploading = screen_uploading_create(lv_scr_act(), uploading_event_handler);
        screen_uploading_update_status(manager.screen_uploading, &manager.status);
        screen_uploading_update_progress(manager.screen_uploading, 0, 0);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Hide all screens
 */
static void hide_all_screens(void) {
    if (manager.screen_boot) screen_boot_set_visible(manager.screen_boot, false);
    if (manager.screen_idle) screen_idle_set_visible(manager.screen_idle, false);
    if (manager.screen_recording) screen_recording_set_visible(manager.screen_recording, false);
    if (manager.screen_uploading) screen_uploading_set_visible(manager.screen_uploading, false);
    if (manager.screen_processing) screen_placeholder_set_visible(manager.screen_processing, false);
    if (manager.screen_downloading) screen_downloading_set_visible(manager.screen_downloading, false);
    if (manager.screen_playing) screen_playing_set_visible(manager.screen_playing, false);  // FIXED: was using placeholder function!
    if (manager.screen_confirmation) screen_placeholder_set_visible(manager.screen_confirmation, false);
    if (manager.screen_queued) screen_queue_set_visible(manager.screen_queued, false);
    if (manager.screen_sleep) screen_sleep_set_visible(manager.screen_sleep, false);
    if (manager.screen_settings) screen_settings_set_visible(manager.screen_settings, false);
    if (manager.screen_error) screen_placeholder_set_visible(manager.screen_error, false);
}

/**
 * @brief Initialize display manager (public API)
 */
bool display_manager_init(display_event_cb_t event_cb) {
    memset(&manager, 0, sizeof(manager));
    manager.event_cb = event_cb;
    manager.current_state = DISPLAY_STATE_BOOT;  // Start in BOOT state
    
    // Default status
    manager.status.battery_percent = 75;
    manager.status.battery_charging = false;
    manager.status.wifi_bars = 3;
    manager.status.wifi_connected = true;
    
    // Create boot screen
    ensure_boot_screen();
    screen_boot_set_visible(manager.screen_boot, true);
    
    return true;
}

/**
 * @brief Switch screen state (public API)
 */
void display_manager_set_state(display_state_t state) {
    if (state == manager.current_state) return;
    
    // Log memory BEFORE transition
    log_memory("before_transition");
    
    // Save old state for cleanup later
    display_state_t old_state = manager.current_state;
    
    // Create/show requested screen (will cover old screen)
    // DON'T hide old screens yet - prevents gray flash
    switch (state) {
        case DISPLAY_STATE_BOOT:
            ensure_boot_screen();
            screen_boot_set_visible(manager.screen_boot, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_IDLE:
            ensure_idle_screen();
            screen_idle_set_visible(manager.screen_idle, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_RECORDING:
            ensure_recording_screen();
            screen_recording_set_visible(manager.screen_recording, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_UPLOADING:
            ensure_uploading_screen();
            screen_uploading_set_visible(manager.screen_uploading, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PROCESSING:
            if (!manager.screen_processing) {
                manager.screen_processing = screen_placeholder_create(lv_scr_act(), "PROCESSING", "AI is thinking...");
            }
            screen_placeholder_set_visible(manager.screen_processing, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_DOWNLOADING:
            if (!manager.screen_downloading) {
                manager.screen_downloading = screen_downloading_create(lv_scr_act(), NULL);
            }
            screen_downloading_set_visible(manager.screen_downloading, true);
            // Update status bar
            screen_downloading_update_status(manager.screen_downloading, &manager.status);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING:
            if (!manager.screen_playing) {
                manager.screen_playing = screen_playing_create(lv_scr_act(), NULL);
            }
            screen_playing_set_visible(manager.screen_playing, true);
            screen_playing_set_mode(manager.screen_playing, 0);  // SAY mode (green)
            // Update status bar
            screen_playing_update_status(manager.screen_playing, &manager.status);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_ASK:
            if (!manager.screen_playing) {
                manager.screen_playing = screen_playing_create(lv_scr_act(), NULL);
            }
            screen_playing_set_visible(manager.screen_playing, true);
            screen_playing_set_mode(manager.screen_playing, 1);  // ASK mode (yellow, mic only)
            // Update status bar
            screen_playing_update_status(manager.screen_playing, &manager.status);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM:
            if (!manager.screen_playing) {
                manager.screen_playing = screen_playing_create(lv_scr_act(), NULL);
            }
            screen_playing_set_visible(manager.screen_playing, true);
            screen_playing_set_mode(manager.screen_playing, 2);  // CONFIRM mode (purple, mic+checkmark)
            // Update status bar
            screen_playing_update_status(manager.screen_playing, &manager.status);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_CONFIRMATION:
            if (!manager.screen_confirmation) {
                manager.screen_confirmation = screen_placeholder_create(lv_scr_act(), "CONFIRM?", "Press button to confirm/cancel");
            }
            screen_placeholder_set_visible(manager.screen_confirmation, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED:
            if (!manager.screen_queued) {
                Serial.println("📋 Creating queue screen...");
                manager.screen_queued = screen_queue_create(lv_scr_act(), queue_event_handler);
                Serial.println("   ✅ Queue screen created");
            }
            screen_queue_set_visible(manager.screen_queued, true);
            // Update status bar and WiFi state
            screen_queue_update_status(manager.screen_queued, &manager.status);
            screen_queue_update_wifi(manager.screen_queued, manager.status.wifi_connected);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_SETTINGS:
            if (!manager.screen_settings) {
                Serial.println("📋 Creating settings screen...");
                manager.screen_settings = screen_settings_create(lv_scr_act(), settings_event_handler);
                Serial.println("   ✅ Settings screen created");
            } else {
                Serial.println("📋 Reusing existing settings screen");
            }
            screen_settings_set_visible(manager.screen_settings, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_SLEEP:
            if (!manager.screen_sleep) {
                manager.screen_sleep = screen_sleep_create(lv_scr_act());
            }
            screen_sleep_set_visible(manager.screen_sleep, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_ERROR:
            if (!manager.screen_error) {
                manager.screen_error = screen_placeholder_create(lv_scr_act(), "ERROR", "Something went wrong");
            }
            screen_placeholder_set_visible(manager.screen_error, true);
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_TEST_CONFIG:
            // Use SETTINGS screen for test config
            if (!manager.screen_settings) {
                manager.screen_settings = screen_placeholder_create(lv_scr_act(), "TEST CONFIG", "Configuration mode");
            }
            screen_placeholder_set_visible(manager.screen_settings, true);
            break;
            
        default:
            // Fallback to IDLE
            ensure_idle_screen();
            screen_idle_set_visible(manager.screen_idle, true);
            state = DISPLAY_STATE_IDLE;
            break;
    }
    
    // Let LVGL render new screen first
    lv_timer_handler();
    
    // Log memory AFTER creating new screen
    log_memory("after_create");
    
    // NOW hide old screen (after new screen is visible)
    switch (old_state) {
        case DISPLAY_STATE_BOOT:
            if (manager.screen_boot) screen_boot_set_visible(manager.screen_boot, false);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_IDLE:
            if (manager.screen_idle) screen_idle_set_visible(manager.screen_idle, false);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_RECORDING:
            if (manager.screen_recording) screen_recording_set_visible(manager.screen_recording, false);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_UPLOADING:
            if (manager.screen_uploading) screen_uploading_set_visible(manager.screen_uploading, false);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_DOWNLOADING:
            if (manager.screen_downloading) screen_downloading_set_visible(manager.screen_downloading, false);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING:
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_ASK:
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM:
            if (manager.screen_playing) screen_playing_set_visible(manager.screen_playing, false);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_SLEEP:
            if (manager.screen_sleep) screen_sleep_set_visible(manager.screen_sleep, false);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED:
            if (manager.screen_queued) screen_queue_set_visible(manager.screen_queued, false);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_SETTINGS:
            if (manager.screen_settings) screen_settings_set_visible(manager.screen_settings, false);
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
    
    // NOW delete old transient screen (after it's hidden)
    // Keep persistent screens: IDLE (galaxy always animating), RECORDING, SLEEP, QUEUE (frequently used)
    // Delete transient screens: BOOT, UPLOADING, DOWNLOADING, PLAYING, SETTINGS, etc.
    switch (old_state) {
        case DISPLAY_STATE_BOOT:
            Serial.println("🧹 Deleting BOOT screen (boot complete)...");
            delete_screen(&manager.screen_boot);
            break;
        // IDLE screen is now persistent - galaxy animation runs in background always
        // case DISPLAY_STATE_IDLE:
        //     Serial.println("🧹 Deleting IDLE screen (frees galaxy animation)...");
        //     delete_screen(&manager.screen_idle);
        //     break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_UPLOADING:
            Serial.println("🧹 Deleting old UPLOADING screen...");
            delete_screen(&manager.screen_uploading);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_DOWNLOADING:
            Serial.println("🧹 Deleting old DOWNLOADING screen...");
            delete_screen(&manager.screen_downloading);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING:
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_ASK:
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM:
            Serial.println("🧹 Deleting old PLAYING screen...");
            delete_screen(&manager.screen_playing);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PROCESSING:
            Serial.println("🧹 Deleting old PROCESSING screen...");
            delete_screen(&manager.screen_processing);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_CONFIRMATION:
            Serial.println("🧹 Deleting old CONFIRMATION screen...");
            delete_screen(&manager.screen_confirmation);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_ERROR:
            Serial.println("🧹 Deleting old ERROR screen...");
            delete_screen(&manager.screen_error);
            break;
        case DISPLAY_STATE_SETTINGS:
            Serial.println("🧹 Deleting old SETTINGS screen...");
            delete_screen(&manager.screen_settings);
            break;
        default:
            // IDLE, RECORDING, SLEEP, QUEUE are persistent - keep them
            break;
    }
    
    // Let LVGL process deletions
    if (old_state != manager.current_state) {
        lv_timer_handler();
        log_memory("after_cleanup");
    }
    
    manager.current_state = state;
}

/**
 * @brief Get current state (public API)
 */
display_state_t display_manager_get_state(void) {
    return manager.current_state;
}

/**
 * @brief Get playing screen object (public API)
 */
lv_obj_t* display_manager_get_playing_screen(void) {
    return manager.screen_playing;
}

/**
 * @brief Get queue screen object (public API)
 */
lv_obj_t* display_manager_get_queue_screen(void) {
    return manager.screen_queued;
}

/**
 * @brief Update status bar (public API)
 */
void display_manager_update_status(const idle_status_t *status) {
    if (!status) return;
    
    // Save status
    manager.status = *status;
    
    // Update visible screen
    if (manager.screen_idle) {
        screen_idle_update_status(manager.screen_idle, &manager.status);
    }
    if (manager.screen_recording) {
        screen_recording_update_status(manager.screen_recording, &manager.status);
    }
    if (manager.screen_uploading) {
        screen_uploading_update_status(manager.screen_uploading, &manager.status);
    }
    if (manager.screen_downloading) {
        screen_downloading_update_status(manager.screen_downloading, &manager.status);
    }
    if (manager.screen_playing) {
        screen_playing_update_status(manager.screen_playing, &manager.status);
    }
    if (manager.screen_queued) {
        screen_queue_update_status(manager.screen_queued, &manager.status);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Update screen data (public API)
 */
void display_manager_update_data(void *data) {
    if (!data) return;
    
    switch (manager.current_state) {
        case DISPLAY_STATE_BOOT:
            // Update boot loading progress
            if (manager.screen_boot) {
                int progress = *(int*)data;
                screen_boot_update_progress(manager.screen_boot, progress);
            }
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_SLEEP:
            // Update sleep screen clock
            // data format: char[2][32] - time_str, date_str
            if (manager.screen_sleep) {
                char (*strs)[32] = (char (*)[32])data;
                screen_sleep_update_time(manager.screen_sleep, strs[0], strs[1]);
            }
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_RECORDING:
            // Update recording duration
            // data format: int - seconds (0-60)
            if (manager.screen_recording) {
                int seconds = *(int*)data;
                screen_recording_update_duration(manager.screen_recording, seconds);
            }
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_UPLOADING:
            // Update upload progress
            // data format: int[2] - sent_kb, total_kb
            if (manager.screen_uploading) {
                int *kb_data = (int*)data;
                screen_uploading_update_progress(manager.screen_uploading, kb_data[0], kb_data[1]);
            }
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_DOWNLOADING:
            // Update download progress
            // data format: int[2] - received_kb, total_kb
            if (manager.screen_downloading) {
                int *kb_data = (int*)data;
                screen_downloading_update_progress(manager.screen_downloading, kb_data[0], kb_data[1]);
            }
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING:
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_ASK:
        case DISPLAY_STATE_PLAYING_CONFIRM:
            // Update playback time
            // data format: int[2] - current_sec, total_sec
            if (manager.screen_playing) {
                int *time_data = (int*)data;
                screen_playing_update_time(manager.screen_playing, time_data[0], time_data[1]);
            }
            break;
            
        case DISPLAY_STATE_SETTINGS:
            // Set initial settings values
            // data format: int[3] - brightness, volume, mic_gain
            if (manager.screen_settings) {
                int *values = (int*)data;
                screen_settings_set_values(manager.screen_settings, values[0], values[1], values[2]);
            }
            break;
            
        default:
            // Other screens: TODO
            break;
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Helper: Update sleep screen clock (public API)
 */
void display_manager_update_sleep_time(const char* time_str, const char* date_str) {
    if (manager.screen_sleep && manager.current_state == DISPLAY_STATE_SLEEP) {
        screen_sleep_update_time(manager.screen_sleep, time_str, date_str);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Helper: Set initial settings values (public API)
 */
void display_manager_set_settings_values(int brightness, int volume, int mic_gain) {
    if (manager.screen_settings && manager.current_state == DISPLAY_STATE_SETTINGS) {
        screen_settings_set_values(manager.screen_settings, brightness, volume, mic_gain);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Helper: Update queue screen filenames list (public API)
 */
void display_manager_update_queue_filenames(const char* filenames) {
    if (manager.screen_queued && manager.current_state == DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED) {
        screen_queue_update_filenames(manager.screen_queued, filenames);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Helper: Update queue screen WiFi state (public API)
 */
void display_manager_update_queue_wifi(bool wifi_connected) {
    if (manager.screen_queued && manager.current_state == DISPLAY_STATE_QUEUED) {
        screen_queue_update_wifi(manager.screen_queued, wifi_connected);
    }
}

[display_recording_ring.cpp - C:\ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-1.8\ESP-IDF-v5.3.2\LVGL_Native_PlatformIO\src\display\display_recording_ring.cpp]

/**
 * @file display_recording_ring.cpp
 * @brief Recording ring animation - DIRECT PIXEL UPDATES for RECORDING screen
 * 
 * Bypasses LVGL for star rendering - writes pixels directly to display.
 * Stars orbit within a 50px wide ring around the mic icon and respond to audio.
 * 
 * Features:
 * - Stars confined to ring around mic icon
 * - Audio-reactive orbit expansion
 * - Direct pixel rendering for blazing performance
 * - Automatically destroyed when leaving RECORDING state
 */

#include "display/display_recording_ring.h"
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <Arduino_GFX_Library.h>
#include <math.h>

// Configuration
#ifndef RING_STAR_COUNT
#define RING_STAR_COUNT 200  // Stars in the ring (visualizer mode)
#endif

// Star structure - orbits within ring
typedef struct {
    // Orbit parameters
    float angle;          // Current angle on orbit (radians)
    float speed;          // Angular velocity (radians/frame)
    float base_radius;    // Base distance from center (within ring bounds)
    
    // Visual properties
    uint16_t base_color;  // Star base color
    float brightness;     // Brightness phase (0-2π for pulsing)
    float brightness_speed; // How fast brightness oscillates
    
    // Position tracking for direct pixel updates
    int16_t x, y;         // Current position
    int16_t prev_x, prev_y;  // Previous position (for erasing)
    bool visible;         // Is this star currently visible?
} ring_star_t;

// Recording ring state
typedef struct {
    ring_star_t stars[RING_STAR_COUNT];  // Stars array
    int cx, cy;           // Center coordinates (mic icon center)
    int inner_r;          // Inner radius of ring
    int outer_r;          // Outer radius of ring
    float audio_level;    // Current audio level (0.0-1.0)
    lv_timer_t *timer;    // Animation timer
} recording_ring_t;

// Global ring instance (needed for direct updates)
static recording_ring_t *g_ring = nullptr;

// UI exclusion zone checker (copied from galaxy)
static bool is_in_ui_zone(int x, int y, int screen_w, int screen_h) {
    // Top status bar: y < 30
    if (y < 30) return true;
    
    // Bottom button area: y > screen_h - 80
    if (y > screen_h - 80) return true;
    
    return false;
}

// Apply brightness to color (copied from galaxy)
static uint16_t apply_brightness(uint16_t color, float brightness_phase) {
    float brightness = 0.5f + 0.5f * sinf(brightness_phase);
    brightness = 0.3f + brightness * 0.7f;  // Range: 0.3 to 1.0
    
    uint8_t r = ((color >> 11) & 0x1F);
    uint8_t g = ((color >> 5) & 0x3F);
    uint8_t b = (color & 0x1F);
    
    r = (uint8_t)(r * brightness);
    g = (uint8_t)(g * brightness);
    b = (uint8_t)(b * brightness);
    
    return ((r & 0x1F) << 11) | ((g & 0x3F) << 5) | (b & 0x1F);
}

/**
 * @brief Update star positions (physics simulation)
 */
static void ring_update_positions(recording_ring_t *r) {
    if (!r) return;
    
    // Audio-reactive expansion: 0.0 = base radius, 1.0 = expand by 200px (aggressive visualizer)
    float expansion = r->audio_level * 200.0f;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < RING_STAR_COUNT; i++) {
        ring_star_t *s = &r->stars[i];
        
        // Save previous position
        s->prev_x = s->x;
        s->prev_y = s->y;
        
        // Update angle
        s->angle += s->speed;
        if (s->angle > 6.28318f) s->angle -= 6.28318f;  // Wrap at 2π
        
        // Calculate current radius (base + audio expansion)
        float current_radius = s->base_radius + expansion;
        
        // Calculate position on circular orbit
        s->x = r->cx + (int16_t)(current_radius * cosf(s->angle));
        s->y = r->cy + (int16_t)(current_radius * sinf(s->angle));
        
        // Update brightness phase
        s->brightness += s->brightness_speed;
        if (s->brightness > 6.28318f) s->brightness -= 6.28318f;  // Wrap at 2π
        
        // Mark as visible if within screen bounds
        s->visible = (s->x >= 0 && s->x < 368 && s->y >= 0 && s->y < 448);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Render stars via direct pixel updates
 */
void recording_ring_render_direct(Arduino_GFX *gfx) {
    if (!g_ring || !gfx) return;
    
    gfx->startWrite();
    
    // Render all stars
    for (int i = 0; i < RING_STAR_COUNT; i++) {
        ring_star_t *s = &g_ring->stars[i];
        
        if (!s->visible) continue;
        
        // Erase old pixel (if different from new)
        if ((s->prev_x != s->x || s->prev_y != s->y) &&
            s->prev_x >= 0 && s->prev_x < 368 && 
            s->prev_y >= 0 && s->prev_y < 448) {
            if (!is_in_ui_zone(s->prev_x, s->prev_y, 368, 448)) {
                gfx->writePixel(s->prev_x, s->prev_y, 0x0000);
            }
        }
        
        // Draw new pixel
        if (s->x >= 0 && s->x < 368 && s->y >= 0 && s->y < 448) {
            if (!is_in_ui_zone(s->x, s->y, 368, 448)) {
                uint16_t color = (s->brightness_speed > 0)
                    ? apply_brightness(s->base_color, s->brightness)
                    : s->base_color;
                gfx->writePixel(s->x, s->y, color);
            }
        }
    }
    
    gfx->endWrite();
}

/**
 * @brief Timer callback - update physics only
 */
static void ring_timer_cb(lv_timer_t *t) {
    recording_ring_t *r = (recording_ring_t*)t->user_data;
    if (!r) return;
    
    ring_update_positions(r);
    // Note: Actual rendering happens in recording_ring_render_direct() after LVGL flush
}

/**
 * @brief Cleanup callback when widget is deleted
 */
static void ring_delete_cb(lv_event_t *e) {
    lv_obj_t *container = lv_event_get_target(e);
    recording_ring_t *r = (recording_ring_t*)lv_obj_get_user_data(container);
    
    if (r) {
        // Stop timer
        if (r->timer) {
            lv_timer_del(r->timer);
            r->timer = NULL;
        }
        
        // Clear global pointer
        if (g_ring == r) {
            g_ring = nullptr;
        }
        
        // Free memory
        lv_mem_free(r);
    }
}

/**
 * @brief Create recording ring widget (public API)
 */
lv_obj_t* recording_ring_create(lv_obj_t *parent, int center_x, int center_y, 
                                int inner_radius, int outer_radius, uint32_t fps) {
    // Create invisible container (just for lifecycle management)
    lv_obj_t *container = lv_obj_create(parent);
    lv_obj_add_flag(container, LV_OBJ_FLAG_HIDDEN);  // Invisible - we render directly
    lv_obj_set_size(container, 368, 448);  // Full screen size
    
    // Allocate ring state
    recording_ring_t *r = (recording_ring_t*)lv_mem_alloc(sizeof(recording_ring_t));
    LV_ASSERT_MALLOC(r);
    memset(r, 0, sizeof(recording_ring_t));
    
    r->cx = center_x;
    r->cy = center_y;
    r->inner_r = inner_radius;
    r->outer_r = outer_radius;
    r->audio_level = 0.0f;
    
    // Initialize stars with random orbits within ring
    float ring_width = outer_radius - inner_radius;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < RING_STAR_COUNT; i++) {
        ring_star_t *s = &r->stars[i];
        
        // Random position within ring
        float radial_pos = (float)lv_rand(0, 1000) / 1000.0f;  // 0.0 to 1.0
        s->base_radius = inner_radius + (radial_pos * ring_width);
        
        // Random starting angle
        s->angle = (lv_rand(0, 3600)) / 572.96f;
        
        // Random speed: -0.03 to 0.03 rad/frame (mix of CW and CCW)
        int speed_sign = (lv_rand(0, 100) < 50) ? 1 : -1;
        s->speed = speed_sign * (0.005f + (lv_rand(0, 250)) / 10000.0f);
        
        // Color variation: mostly white, some yellowish, some blueish
        int color_choice = lv_rand(0, 100);
        if (color_choice < 60) {
            s->base_color = 0xFFFF;  // White
        } else if (color_choice < 85) {
            s->base_color = 0xFFE0;  // Yellowish
        } else {
            s->base_color = 0xADFF;  // Blueish
        }
        
        // Brightness pulsing: 50% static, 50% pulsing
        if (lv_rand(0, 100) < 50) {
            s->brightness = (lv_rand(0, 628)) / 100.0f;  // Random phase
            s->brightness_speed = 0.02f + (lv_rand(0, 300)) / 10000.0f;  // 0.02-0.05 rad/frame
        } else {
            s->brightness = 0.0f;
            s->brightness_speed = 0.0f;  // Static brightness
        }
        
        // Initialize position
        s->x = center_x + (int16_t)(s->base_radius * cosf(s->angle));
        s->y = center_y + (int16_t)(s->base_radius * sinf(s->angle));
        s->prev_x = s->x;
        s->prev_y = s->y;
        s->visible = true;
    }
    
    // Store state in container
    lv_obj_set_user_data(container, r);
    
    // Register cleanup callback
    lv_obj_add_event_cb(container, ring_delete_cb, LV_EVENT_DELETE, r);
    
    // Create animation timer
    r->timer = lv_timer_create(ring_timer_cb, 1000 / fps, r);
    
    // Set as global instance
    g_ring = r;
    
    return container;
}

/**
 * @brief Set GFX instance for direct rendering (no-op, kept for API compatibility)
 */
void recording_ring_set_gfx(Arduino_GFX *gfx) {
    // No-op: gfx is passed directly to recording_ring_render_direct()
    (void)gfx;
}

/**
 * @brief Set audio amplitude for reactive orbit expansion
 */
void recording_ring_set_audio(float audio_level) {
    if (!g_ring) return;
    
    // Clamp and smooth the amplitude
    audio_level = LV_CLAMP(0.0f, audio_level, 1.0f);
    
    // Smooth transition (exponential moving average) - fast response for visualizer
    const float smoothing = 0.7f;  // Higher = faster response (visualizer needs responsiveness)
    g_ring->audio_level = g_ring->audio_level * (1.0f - smoothing) + audio_level * smoothing;
}
